Motivation: 在通量泵浦约瑟夫森电路中，当两个谐振简正模参与同一个驱动SQUID坐标时，会出现占据数依赖的配对相互作用。这种相互作用中，控制模式虽然不交换激发，但能够改变信号模式配对跃迁的共振频率和强度。理解这种相互作用的物理起源及其在玻色子量子控制中的应用潜力，是发展多模玻色子控制平台的关键需求。

Method: 从完整的约瑟夫森余弦哈密顿量出发，采用含时Schrieffer-Wolff变换和Magnus展开两种互补的理论方法，揭示产生占据数依赖配对相互作用的直接过程和虚过程。通过数值收敛的Floquet方法处理未展开的双模电路哈密顿量，对有效理论进行基准测试和验证。分析了对角约瑟夫森非线性（引起配对共振频移）和非线性约瑟夫森矩阵元（使avoided-crossing间隙本身依赖于控制模占据数）这两种不同物理起源的贡献。

Result: 占据数依赖性产生了二维配对能谱，能够在玻色Fock格子内实现选择性跃迁。当控制模式制备在相干叠加态时，条件动力学转化为信号-控制模式纠缠和控制模式依赖的非高斯态生成。可观测量层面的对比显示，配对跃迁强度的变化无法仅由占据数依赖的共振频移来重现，证实了非线性矩阵元效应的必要性。数值Floquet计算与有效理论高度吻合，验证了理论框架的准确性。

Conclusion: 建立了玻色子控制的参数化相互作用机制，其中一个谐振模式的量子态可以同时控制另一个模式中非线性过程的谱位置和耦合强度。这种双重控制来源于约瑟夫森非线性的两个不同方面：对角非线性和非线性矩阵元，二者缺一不可。该机制为通量泵浦约瑟夫森电路提供了灵活的参数光子生成和多模玻色子量子控制平台，可用于实现复杂的量子态工程和纠缠生成。

Abstract: Flux-pumped Josephson circuits provide a versatile platform for parametric photon generation and multimode bosonic control. We identify an occupation-conditioned pair interaction that arises when two harmonic normal modes participate in the same driven SQUID coordinate. We consider a degenerate pair process in which two photons are created or annihilated in a signal mode while the occupation of a second harmonic mode remains unchanged. Although it does not exchange excitations, this controller mode modifies both the resonance frequency and the strength of the pair transition. The two effects have different physical origins: diagonal Josephson nonlinearities shift the pair resonance, whereas the nonlinear Josephson matrix element makes the avoided-crossing gap itself depend on the controller occupation. Starting from the full Josephson cosine, we develop complementary time-dependent Schrieffer--Wolff and Magnus descriptions that expose the direct and virtual processes responsible for this interaction, and benchmark the resulting effective theory against a numerically converged Floquet treatment of the unexpanded two-mode circuit Hamiltonian. The occupation dependence produces a two-dimensional pair spectrum that enables selective transitions within the bosonic Fock lattice. When the controller is prepared in a coherent superposition, the same interaction converts this conditional dynamics into signal--controller entanglement and controller-dependent non-Gaussian state generation. Observable-level comparisons further show that the variation of the pair-transition strength cannot be reproduced by occupation-dependent resonance shifts alone. These results establish a boson-controlled parametric interaction in which the state of one harmonic mode controls both the spectral position and the coupling strength of a nonlinear process in another.

</details>


### [104] [In-plane optimal qubit operation with control fidelities exceeding 99 %](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33229)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Zhenzhen Kong,Rong-Long Ma,Hao-Tian Jiang,Ao-Ran Li,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Tao Wu,Rui Zheng,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了平面锗量子点中单空穴自旋量子比特的研究,通过优化面内磁场方向找到最佳工作点,将Hahn回波相干时间提升5倍,控制保真度提高近10倍,最高门保真度达99.82%,为可扩展量子信息处理提供了重要进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于半导体量子点的空穴自旋量子比特可通过全电学操控,在构建大规模量子计算机方面具有前景。然而,价带空穴丰富的物理机制导致量子比特性质具有各向异性。研究者希望延长相干时间并实现高保真度的量子比特操控,因此需要系统研究空穴自旋量子比特对电荷噪声的各向异性敏感性,并找到最优工作点以最小化噪声影响。

Method: 在平面锗量子点中制备单空穴自旋量子比特,系统研究其在面内磁场下对电荷噪声的各向异性敏感性。通过关联纵向自旋-电耦合敏感度与量子比特的相干性能和控制性能,识别出电荷噪声敏感性最小化的最佳工作点。通过优化磁场方向来降低自旋-电耦合敏感度,并在最佳工作点使用门集合层析技术(gate set tomography)评估门操作保真度。

Result: 通过优化磁场方向,成功将Hahn回波相干时间提升了5倍,控制不保真度抑制了近10倍。在确定的最佳工作点处,门集合层析技术测得最高门保真度为99.82%。实验结果证实了自旋-电耦合敏感度的降低与量子比特性能提升之间的直接关联。

Conclusion: 本研究成功证明了通过优化面内磁场方向可以显著降低锗量子点空穴自旋量子比特对电荷噪声的敏感性,从而大幅提升相干时间和门操作保真度。在最佳工作点实现的99.82%门保真度表明空穴自旋量子比特在可扩展量子信息处理中具有巨大潜力,为未来构建大规模量子计算机提供了重要的技术路径。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits based on semiconductor quantum dots are promising for building future large-scale quantum computers owing to their all-electrical manipulation. However, abundant physical mechanisms of valence band holes lead to anisotropic qubit properties. There is an opportunity to prolong the coherence time and achieve high-fidelity qubit manipulations. Here, we report a single-hole spin qubit in a planar germanium quantum dot and investigate its anisotropic susceptibility to charge noise under an in-plane magnetic field. By correlating the longitudinal spin-electric susceptibility with qubit coherence and control performance, we identify an optimal operating point where the sensitivity to charge noise is minimized. We find that optimizing the magnetic-field orientation reduces the spin-electric susceptibility, resulting in a five-fold enhancement of the Hahn-echo coherence time and a nearly tenfold suppression of control infidelity. At the optimal operating point, gate set tomography demonstrates the maximum gate fidelity of 99.82 %. Our finding enhances the prospects of hole spin qubits for scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [105] [High-fidelity geometric quantum gates exceeding 99.9% in germanium quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33251)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Rong-Long Ma,Zhenzhen Kong,Ao-Ran Li,Chengxian Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yang Liu,Hao-Tian Jiang,Zhi-Tao Wu,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Guang-Can Guo,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在锗量子点中实现了单空穴自旋量子比特，并通过几何量子计算方法实现了抗噪声的高保真度量子比特操控，最高保真度超过99.9%，为半导体量子计算中可重复实现高保真度操控提供了有效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高保真度和鲁棒的量子比特操控是容错量子计算的关键要求。传统门操作在连续I门操作期间受到离共振噪声的严重限制，导致量子比特性能下降。因此需要开发一种能够抵抗噪声、可重复实现高保真度操控的方法。

Method: 研究者在锗量子点中构建单空穴自旋量子比特，使用门集层析技术表征控制保真度。针对发现的离共振噪声问题，引入几何量子计算方法来实现抗噪声的量子比特操控。通过在宽范围的拉比频率下测试几何门，并在微波频率失谐条件下评估门保真度，验证几何方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 常规门操作的最高控制保真度分别为：I门97.48%，X/2门99.81%，Y/2门99.88%。几何门控制保真度在宽拉比频率范围内保持在99%以上，最高保真度超过99.9%。当微波频率失谐±2.5 MHz时，几何X/2和Y/2门保真度仍超过99%；当频率失谐±1.2 MHz时，I门保真度同样超过99%，充分展示了抗噪声特性。

Conclusion: 研究证明几何量子计算是在半导体量子计算中可重复实现高保真度量子比特操控的潜在有效方法。该方法通过几何相位实现对噪声的固有抵抗能力，显著提升了量子门操作的鲁棒性和保真度，为推进容错量子计算的发展提供了重要技术路径。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity and robust qubit manipulations is a crucial requirement for realizing faulttolerant quantum computation. Here, we demonstrate a single-hole spin qubit in a germanium quantum dot and characterize its control fidelity using gate set tomography. The maximum control fidelities reach 97.48%, 99.81%, 99.88% for the I, X/2 and Y /2 gate, respectively. These results reveal that off-resonance noise during consecutive I gates in gate set tomography sequences severely limits qubit performance. Therefore, we introduce geometric quantum computation to realize noiseresilient qubit manipulation. The geometric gate control fidelities remain above 99% across a wide range of Rabi frequencies. The maximum fidelity surpasses 99.9%. Furthermore, the fidelities of geometric X/2 and Y /2 (I) gates exceed 99% even when detuning the microwave frequency by +-2.5 MHz (+-1.2 MHz), highlighting the noise-resilient feature. These results demonstrate that geometric quantum computation is a potential method for achieving high-fidelity qubit manipulation reproducibly in semiconductor quantum computation.

</details>


### [106] [NxM-Version Programming for Quantum Software: High-Level Components across Frameworks and Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33255)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Higor Amario de Souza,Anthony Accioly,Valter Vieira de Camargo,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Quanifi系统,将多个量子软件框架（Qiskit、Cirq等）封装为Apache NiFi数据流组件,通过OpenQASM 2.0实现跨框架电路交换,并引入N×M程序执行方法(N种实现×M个执行引擎)进行冗余测试,成功发现并报告了三个真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件开发需要掌握量子门、量子比特以及多种框架特定知识(Qiskit、Cirq、Qrisp、pyQuil、PennyLane等),各框架通过独立工具链访问量子硬件,缺乏统一性和互操作性。现有开发流程难以系统化地组织量子软件的构建、测试和运行,需要一种更高层次的抽象和跨框架协作机制来提升量子软件工程的效率和可靠性。

Method: 设计并实现Quanifi系统,将各量子框架的高级例程封装为Apache NiFi数据流画布上的组件;采用OpenQASM 2.0作为中间表示格式实现跨框架电路交换;提出N×M程序执行方法,在单个NiFi流中对N种算法实现和M个执行引擎(模拟器或真实硬件)进行笛卡尔积式交叉测试;通过标记每个结果的实现来源和执行引擎,建立结果差异与具体缺陷源的可追溯映射关系。

Result: 使用三个框架构建的Grover算法在IBM、IQM和Quantum Inspire的三台量子计算机上运行;三种加法器构建方法通过两条路径在一台IQM设备上执行。N×M执行矩阵成功发现三个真实缺陷:Quantum Inspire适配器中的错误门集声明、Tuna-17上与Rx角度取反一致的执行行为、Open Quantum进行的电路修改(在不报错的情况下返回错误答案)。所有缺陷均已报告,Open Quantum提供商确认并修复了其QASM解析缺陷。

Conclusion: Quanifi通过Apache NiFi实现量子软件框架的统一集成和跨框架互操作,提供了可视化数据流编程范式。N×M程序执行方法通过系统化的冗余测试有效识别量子软件栈中的实现错误、硬件问题和特定组合缺陷,证明了跨框架、跨平台测试在量子软件质量保证中的实用价值。该方法为量子软件工程提供了新的测试和验证手段,有助于提升量子计算生态系统的可靠性和成熟度。

Abstract: Quantum computing has in recent years evolved from a purely theoretical field to an active area in both academia and industry. As a result, Quantum Software Engineering has emerged as an area that aims to organize the process of building, testing, and running quantum software. However, writing quantum software still requires programming with quantum gates and qubits, as well as knowledge specific to quantum software frameworks such as Qiskit, Cirq, Qrisp, pyQuil and PennyLane, each of which reaches quantum hardware through its own toolchain. We present Quanifi, which packages the high-level routines of these frameworks as components on the Apache NiFi dataflow canvas. The components exchange circuits as OpenQASM 2.0, so a circuit built by one framework can be executed by another. This allows for two types of redundancy: several frameworks implement the same algorithm, and several engines (simulators or real hardware) execute the same circuit. We describe N$\times$M program execution, which crosses the two: a single NiFi flow runs each of the $N$ implementations on each of the $M$ engines, and because every result is labelled with its implementation and its engine, a disagreement can be linked to an implementation, to an engine, or to a single implementation-engine pair. We ran Grover's algorithm built by three frameworks on three quantum computers from IBM, IQM and Quantum Inspire, and a matrix of three adder builders on one IQM device reached through two routes. N$\times$M execution helped us discover three real defects: an incorrect gate-set declaration in the Quantum Inspire adapter, execution consistent with negated $R_x$ angles on Tuna-17, and circuit modification through Open Quantum. The latter two returned wrong answers without reporting an error. We reported all three defects and Open Quantum's provider confirmed and fixed its QASM parsing defect.

</details>


### [107] [Preparation-controlled relaxation in an overdamped RLC circuit: A pedagogical route to the spectral Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33277)
*Matheus H. dos Santos,Cler T. Garcez,Jeveson C. da Silva,G. D. de Moraes Neto,Norton G. de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过过阻尼RLC电路研究了经典和量子系统中的"姆潘巴效应"(Mpemba effect)——初始能量更高的系统可能比低能量系统更快冷却。研究揭示了这一现象的关键机制:弛豫路径不仅由初始能量决定,还取决于慢、快衰减模式的激发强度,这由初始电荷和电流设定。论文从确定性系统扩展到随机开放系统,并进一步探讨了量子谐振子中的类似现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为高能量系统应该比低能量系统冷却得慢,但"姆潘巴效应"挑战了这一直觉。尽管该效应在水的反常冷却中被观察到,但其物理机制尚不明确。本研究旨在通过可实验验证的经典RLC电路系统,建立一个清晰的理论框架来理解能量顺序反转现象,并将其推广到随机系统和量子系统,揭示这一普遍现象背后的深层物理机制。

Method: 研究采用多层次方法:(1)对经典过阻尼RLC电路,推导模态制备条件和交叉判据,通过示波器轨迹重构电荷、电流和能量来实验验证;(2)将分析扩展到由外部白噪声驱动的随机电路;(3)采用平衡态Ornstein-Uhlenbeck处理,区分系综平均携带的信息与协方差存储的信息,研究不同高斯态制备;(4)通过Fokker-Planck生成器过渡到马尔可夫量子动力学;(5)研究热阻尼量子谐振子和亚阈值参量振荡器,使用Liouvillian形式描述弛豫过程对生成器谱、制备激发的模式和可观测量可见模式的依赖关系。

Result: 实验和理论结果表明:(1)经典过阻尼RLC电路确实展现了能量交叉现象,初始能量更高的状态可以在弛豫过程中降至低能量状态之下;(2)该效应的关键在于初始条件(电荷和电流)对慢、快衰减模式的不同激发;(3)能量相等的状态可以沿不同路径弛豫,取决于其在相空间中的几何配置;(4)白噪声驱动下该反转效应依然存在;(5)对于热阻尼量子谐振子,有限平均占据数下平均裸能量过剩以单指数衰减,禁止能量顺序反转;(6)亚阈值参量振荡器恢复慢、快正交扇区,允许由位移和协方差控制的交叉现象。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了从经典到量子系统的统一框架来理解反常弛豫现象(姆潘巴效应的谱类比)。核心洞见是:弛豫动力学不仅由初始能量决定,还取决于系统不同衰减模式的初始激发方式。在经典RLC电路中,这通过初始电荷和电流的特定组合实现;在量子系统中,需要适当的哈密顿量结构(如参量振荡器)来支持可区分的快慢扇区。Liouvillian表述揭示了观测到的弛豫行为是生成器谱、制备激发的模式和可观测量选择的共同结果。这为设计和控制非平衡弛豫过程提供了新的物理图景和实验平台。

Abstract: Can a system with more stored energy cool faster than one with less? An overdamped resistor--inductor--capacitor (RLC) circuit offers a classical, experimentally accessible spectral Mpemba analogue: both states lose energy monotonically, yet the initially higher-energy state can cross below the lower-energy one. The reason is that initial energy alone does not determine the relaxation path; initial charge and current set how strongly the slow and fast decay modes are excited. We develop this mechanism from deterministic to stochastic and open-system descriptions. For the classical circuit, we derive the modal preparation conditions and crossing criterion, explain geometrically why equal-energy states can relax differently, and confirm the effect experimentally by reconstructing charge, current, and energy from oscilloscope traces. The inversion survives when the circuit is driven by externally injected white noise. An equilibrium Ornstein--Uhlenbeck treatment separates information carried by the ensemble mean from that stored in its covariance: centered Gibbs preparations preserve mean-energy ordering, while displaced or anisotropic Gaussian states can encode distinct slow and fast sectors. A Fokker--Planck generator then bridges to Markovian quantum dynamics. For a thermally damped quantum harmonic oscillator, the mean bare-energy excess decays with a single exponential factor, forbidding energy-order inversion for finite mean occupation. A below-threshold parametric oscillator restores slow and fast quadrature sectors, allowing crossings controlled by displacement and covariance. The Liouvillian formulation shows that observed relaxation depends jointly on the generator spectrum, the modes populated by preparation, and the modes visible to the observable.

</details>


### [108] [Temperature--Hamiltonian Ambiguity in Strong-Coupling Quantum Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33389)
*Jinshuang Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强耦合系统中局域温度和哈密顿量的定义问题,提出了温度-哈密顿量不确定性概念,并通过可解模型给出了基于独立物理信息确定局域热力学量的操作性方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强耦合条件下,子系统会偏离其裸哈密顿量在热浴温度下的吉布斯态。约化平衡态虽然固定了无量纲吉布斯生成元,但无法唯一确定吉布斯表示中的局域哈密顿量和温度,存在"温度-哈密顿量不确定性"。需要建立超越弱耦合近似的局域热力学操作性框架,以区分态表示与热力学赋值。

Method: 提出操作性解决方案:通过独立于稳态吉布斯参数化的物理信息来识别局域哈密顿量。采用两个精确可解模型验证方案:(1)二能级系统-振子模型,展示基于指定局域哈密顿量的温度重整化与在热浴温度下的平均力哈密顿量能级重整化的等价描述;(2)玻色子模型,通过延迟动力学独立固定重整化的局域激发尺度,从而在稳态布居数确定温度之前先确定局域哈密顿量。

Result: 精确可解模型明确展示了温度-哈密顿量不确定性:二能级系统-振子模型实现了态等价的不同描述方式,玻色子模型成功通过动力学信息独立确定重整化局域激发尺度和哈密顿量。当精确约化态与独立确定的哈密顿量生成的吉布斯族兼容时,可唯一确定局域温度。

Conclusion: 成功区分了态表示与热力学赋值,为强耦合条件下的局域热力学提供了操作性框架。通过引入独立物理信息(如动力学性质)来打破温度-哈密顿量不确定性,实现了局域温度和哈密顿量的唯一性定义,拓展了超越弱耦合极限的开放量子系统热力学理论。

Abstract: Strong coupling generally drives a subsystem away from the Gibbs state of its bare Hamiltonian at the bath temperature. We formulate a temperature--Hamiltonian ambiguity: the reduced equilibrium state fixes the dimensionless Gibbs generator, but does not by itself uniquely determine the local Hamiltonian and temperature entering a Gibbs representation. We propose an operational resolution in which the local Hamiltonian is identified from physical information independent of the stationary Gibbs parametrization. Whenever the exact reduced state is compatible with the Gibbs family generated by that independently identified Hamiltonian, it then determines a unique local temperature. An exactly solvable two-level-system--oscillator model makes the ambiguity explicit through state-equivalent descriptions based on temperature renormalization relative to a specified local Hamiltonian and Hamiltonian-of-mean-force level renormalization at the bath temperature. An exact bosonic model provides the complementary case in which retarded dynamics independently fixes a renormalized local excitation scale and thereby specifies the local Hamiltonian before the stationary populations determine the temperature. These results distinguish state representation from thermodynamic assignment and provide an operational framework for local thermodynamics beyond weak coupling.

</details>


### [109] [Local Many Worlds in Spacetime: Deutsch-Hayden Descriptors and Local Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33453)
*Mordecai Waegell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了贝尔定理要求局域物理理论必须是多世界理论,提出了量子力学的局域多世界形式体系,并展示了如何从Deutsch-Hayden描述符直接构造局域波函数,从而结合薛定谔图像和海森堡图像的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Everett多世界诠释使用非可分的普适波函数,缺乏时空可分性;而局域海森堡图像中的Deutsch-Hayden描述符虽然紧凑但较为晦涩难懂。需要一种方法能够结合局域薛定谔图像的直观物理叙述性和海森堡图像的紧凑性,使局域多世界理论更易理解和应用。

Method: 文章采用局域多世界形式体系,在薛定谔图像中,每个时空事件拥有自己的局域波函数,仅由其过去光锥内的局域波函数和局域幺正演化算符构建;在海森堡图像中,使用Deutsch-Hayden描述符描述每个独立系统。核心方法是建立从DH描述符直接构造任意N量子比特系统局域波函数的数学框架,从而在两种图像之间建立明确的对应关系。

Result: 成功证明了对于任意N量子比特系统,可以直接从Deutsch-Hayden描述符构造出相应的局域波函数。这使得研究者能够利用紧凑的DH描述符进行计算,同时获得直观的局域波函数物理图像。两种表述包含完全相同的信息,但各有优势:局域波函数提供清晰直观的物理叙述,而描述符形式更为紧凑高效。

Conclusion: 局域多世界理论提供了一个满足洛伦兹协变因果结构的时空可分物理理论框架,既保留了局域幺正演化和概率流守恒,又避免了波函数坍缩。通过建立从DH描述符到局域波函数的直接构造方法,本文使局域海森堡图像变得更易于理解和使用,成功结合了两种图像的优点,为局域多世界理论的应用提供了更完整和实用的工具。

Abstract: Bell's theorem establishes that a local and spatially separable physical theory with no superdeterminism or retrocausality must be a theory of many local worlds. The local many worlds formalism of quantum mechanics is a transparent separable physical theory in spacetime with a Lorentz covariant causal structure. Unlike the Everett many worlds interpretation, there is no nonseparable universal wavefunction in configuration space, but like Everett, there is only local unitary evolution which conserves probability (fluid) current, and no wavefunction collapse. Each event in spacetime has its own local wavefunction which is built up using only the local wavefunctions and local unitary evolution operators within its past light cone, and which is entirely disconnected from spacelike separated local wavefunctions. We call this the local Schrödinger picture. Local many worlds can also be described in the Heisenberg picture by ascribing Deutsch-Hayden (DH) descriptors to each individual system, each of which evolves under local unitary operations. Both the local wavefunctions and DH descriptors contain records of past interactions, and while the local wavefunctions provide a clear and intuitive physical narrative, the descriptors contain all of the same information in a much more compact form. Unfortunately, the compact form is also somewhat cryptic, which has made the local Heisenberg picture relatively inaccessible. The purpose of this article is to show that the local wavefunctions for any $N$-qubit system can always be constructed directly from the DH descriptors, allowing us to combine the benefits of both pictures.

</details>


### [110] [How many photons can a molecule scatter before its coherence is lost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33475)
*M. R. Tarbutt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了保守光阱中分子量子比特因散射俘获激光光子而导致的退相干问题,推导了退相干速率公式,并指出可通过选择使矢量和张量极化率同时较小的特定波长来抑制分子的退相干,以CaF分子为例进行了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子量子比特在光阱中会因散射俘获激光光子而失去相干性,这一退相干过程对分子的影响通常远大于原子。需要深入理解退相干机制并找到抑制方法,以提高分子量子比特的相干时间,这对基于分子的量子信息处理至关重要。

Method: 通过理论计算分析光阱中分子量子比特的退相干速率,将退相干率分解为两部分:一部分等于两个量子比特态的平均拉曼散射速率,另一部分与两态瑞利散射振幅差值的平方成正比。利用分子极化率的矢量部分(α₁)和张量部分(α₂)来表示退相干速率,分析在特定波长下α₁和α₂同时较小时退相干被抑制的条件,并针对CaF分子识别满足条件的特定波长。

Result: 理论推导表明退相干速率可用分子极化率的矢量和张量分量表示,证明了在α₁和α₂都较小的特定波长处退相干速率会被显著抑制。针对CaF分子,成功识别出了能够实现退相干抑制的特定激光波长,为实验选择提供了理论指导。

Conclusion: 保守光阱中分子量子比特的退相干主要由拉曼散射和瑞利散射差异引起,虽然分子的退相干速率通常远高于原子,但可以通过精心选择使矢量和张量极化率同时最小化的激光波长来有效抑制退相干。该研究为CaF等分子量子比特系统的实验实现提供了重要的理论依据和波长选择策略。

Abstract: Molecular qubits held in conservative optical traps may lose coherence when they scatter photons from the trapping lasers. The rate for this process has two parts, one equal to the mean Raman scattering rate of the two qubit states, and the other proportional to the square of the difference in their Rayleigh scattering amplitudes. This paper calculates the decoherence rate, expressing results in terms of the vector and tensor parts of the molecular polarizability, $α_1$ and $α_2$. While the decoherence rate is generally much larger for molecules than for atoms, it is suppressed at wavelengths where $α_1$ and $α_2$ are both small. The wavelengths where this occurs are identified for CaF molecules.

</details>


### [111] [The Fully Depolarizing Noise Conjecture for Entangled Physical States: A Twenty-Year Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33508)
*Gil Kalai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了作者2006年提出的关于纠缠物理量子比特中相关错误的猜想，该猜想认为物理量子计算机中纠缠量子比特会受到联合完全退极化噪声的影响，这可能对可扩展量子容错构成重大挑战。文章还讨论了噪声敏感性、计算复杂性以及量子优势实验声明的统计分析等相关研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子容错理论通常基于标准噪声模型，但作者在2006年提出了一个更具结构性约束的猜想：纠缠物理量子比特中存在联合完全退极化噪声分量，其速率与双量子比特门错误率相当。该猜想若成立，将对可扩展量子计算的容错能力构成根本性障碍。随着实验量子计算的进展，该猜想现已具备在现有设备上进行实证检验的条件，因此有必要重新审视这一问题。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与实验可行性评估相结合的方法。作者回顾了2006年提出的相关错误猜想的理论基础，分析了该猜想超越标准噪声模型的结构性特征。同时，作者评估了利用当前量子计算设备对该猜想进行实证检验的可能性。此外，文章还从更广泛的视角讨论了噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）系统中的噪声敏感性和计算复杂性问题，以及量子优势实验声明的统计分析方法。

Result: 作者指出该猜想仍然是开放性问题，尚未得到证明或证伪。不过，近期实验量子计算技术的进步使得在现有量子设备上对该猜想进行实证测试成为可能。文章还阐述了作者在量子计算批判性研究中的两个相关方向：一是噪声中等规模量子系统中噪声敏感性与计算复杂性的作用；二是对量子优势实验声明的统计分析。这些研究方向共同构成了对量子计算实际可行性的系统性审视。

Conclusion: 相关错误猜想对量子容错理论提出了重要挑战，该猜想假设的联合退极化噪声如果在物理系统中普遍存在，将对可扩展量子计算构成严重障碍。虽然该猜想仍未解决，但当前量子计算实验技术的发展为验证这一猜想提供了新的契机。作者强调需要继续关注量子系统中的噪声结构、噪声敏感性以及量子优势声明的严格统计验证，这些都是评估量子计算实际潜力的关键问题。文章同时包含了对Yuri Gurevich的致敬性个人回顾。

Abstract: In this paper I revisit my 2006 conjecture on correlated errors in entangled physical qubits, originally proposed as a potential obstruction to quantum fault tolerance. The conjecture asserts that, in any physical implementation of a quantum computer, the effective noise channel acting on entangled physical qubits contains a joint fully depolarizing component, with a rate comparable to that of two-qubit gate errors. This hypothesized structural constraint goes beyond standard noise models and, if valid, would pose a significant challenge to scalable quantum fault tolerance.
  The conjecture remains open, but recent advances in experimental quantum computing bring it within reach of empirical testing on current devices. I also discuss two related directions in my critical study of quantum computation: the role of noise sensitivity and computational complexity in noisy intermediate-scale quantum systems, and the statistical analysis of experimental claims of quantum advantage. Finally, since this paper is written for a volume honoring Yuri Gurevich, I include some reflections on the ways in which my scientific and personal trajectory became intertwined with Yuri's.

</details>


### [112] [Engineering Defect-Phonon Interactions Through Heterophase-Interfaces in Silicon Carbide Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33545)
*Kirlie Iulius Figuera Michal,Jin Hee Lee,Keiju Sato,Takuji Maekawa,Je-Hyung Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用异质相界面工程来调控宽带隙半导体中点缺陷的声子相互作用,在3C/4H碳化硅异质界面中实现了室温下零声子线跃迁的显著增强,为制备高亮度室温量子发射器提供了新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带隙半导体中的点缺陷可作为自旋和光子量子比特,但固有的电子-声子相互作用导致宽的声子边带发射和弱的零声子线跃迁,限制了高效自旋-光子界面和可扩展光子介导的相互作用。传统上堆垛层错被视为需要消除的缺陷,研究者希望探索晶相界面是否能提供额外的自由度来调控缺陷-声子相互作用,超越单晶的固有性质。

Method: 采用基于远程外延技术的可控异质相界面作为晶体异质性工程策略。通过构建3C/4H碳化硅异质界面结构,将硅空位(Vsi)缺陷引入该界面体系中,并与单相4H-碳化硅薄膜中的缺陷进行对比研究,表征其光学跃迁性质和零声子线特性。

Result: 与单相4H-碳化硅薄膜相比,3C/4H异质界面碳化硅中的硅空位表现出零声子光学跃迁的显著增强。即使在室温条件下,零声子线线宽也大幅收窄至4.71 meV。实验结果证明晶相界面确实可以提供调控缺陷-声子相互作用的额外自由度。

Conclusion: 异质相界面工程被证实是调控缺陷-声子相互作用的有效途径,能够实现具有增强零声子光学跃迁特性的高亮度室温量子发射器。该研究突破了传统将堆垛层错视为缺陷的观念,为宽带隙半导体量子器件的性能优化开辟了新方向,对量子信息处理、通信和传感领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Point defects in wide-bandgap semiconductors offer spin and photonic qubits in a solid-state platform, making them important building blocks for quantum information processing, communication, and sensing. While these systems have the strong advantage of room-temperature operation, intrinsic electron-phonon interaction induces broad phonon-sideband emission and weak zero-phonon-line transitions, limiting efficient spin-photon interfaces and scalable photon-mediated interaction. Here, we introduce a controlled heterophase interface based on the remote epitaxy technique as a crystal heterogeneity-engineering strategy. While stacking faults are treated as imperfections to be eliminated, our results instead show that crystal-phase interfaces can provide an additional degree of freedom to engineer defect-phonon interaction beyond the intrinsic properties of a single crystal. In comparison with single-phase 4H-silicon carbide (SiC) membranes, Vsi in 3C/4H heterointerface SiC exhibits a drastic enhancement of zero-phonon optical transitions, with a substantially narrower zero-phonon linewidth of 4.71 meV even at room temperature. These results establish heterophase-interface engineering as an effective route for tailoring defect-phonon interactions and realizing bright room-temperature quantum emitters with enhanced zero-phonon optical transitions.

</details>


### [113] [Terminal-Register Certification for Finite-Measurement Learning of Multiscale Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33567)
*Bhvain Makwana,Kashyap Patel,Manjunath Joshi,Jaideep Mulherkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用逆二进制多尺度纠缠重整化张量网络(MERA)学习一维量子态的方法,提出了噪声感知的有限采样认证定理,并通过横场Ising模型基态的数值实验验证了MERA架构相比局域电路在保真度、长程误差等指标上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态学习不仅需要具有表达能力的拟设(ansatz),还需要在有限测量和非完美实现条件下仍然有效的操作性认证方法。现有方法在面对测量噪声、有限采样和实现误差时缺乏理论保证,因此需要开发一种结合MERA架构优势和噪声感知认证的量子态学习协议。

Method: 采用逆MERA作为量子态学习的拟设,在粗粒化过程中相干控制被移除的量子比特并在终端寄存器统一测量;证明理想的顺序测量与终端测量在匹配因果操作下产生相同的比特串分布;提出噪声感知定理,为有限采样认证引入经过校准的总变差实现预算;使用开边界横场Ising模型基态进行协议评估,并通过多起点精确梯度诊断和因果锥完备的参数匹配局域电路对比来验证MERA的架构公平性。

Result: 在8量子比特冻结调度中,使用每次运行5.6亿次模拟训练测量,在全部60次留出测试中实现了超过0.99的保真度,平均保真度达到0.996886;1080个电路噪声单元和6480个置信度-覆盖率行通过固定电路鲁棒性验证,无可靠性违规;在16量子比特的120次精确梯度多起点诊断中,MERA在60对配对重启中有58对保真度更高,全部60对长程误差更低;因果锥完备的局域电路尽管聚合门暴露量高出2.62倍,但在全部30对比较中均输于MERA(保真度、长程误差、能量和熵四个指标)。

Conclusion: MERA架构在一维量子态学习中展现出显著优势,通过噪声感知的有限采样认证方法可以在实际条件下实现高保真度的量子态制备。实验结果证实MERA相比局域电路具有更好的保真度和长程关联捕获能力,即使在更低的门操作暴露下也能达到更优性能,验证了张量网络启发架构在量子机器学习中的有效性和架构公平性。

Abstract: Structured quantum-state learning not only depends on an expressive ansatz but also on an operational certificate that stays meaningful with finite measurements and imperfect implementation. We study pure one dimensional states learning by an inverse binary multiscale entanglement renormalization ansatz (MERA). In the learning procedure, the qubits removed during coarse graining are controlled coherently and measured together at the terminal register. We confirm that an ideal sequential and terminal measurement schedule delivers the same complete bit string distribution under matched causal operations, while normalized postselection can amplify perturbations inversely with prefix acceptance. A noise aware theorem introduces an individual calibrated total variation implementation budget to the finite shot certificate. The protocol is estimated on an open boundary transverse field Ising ground state. A frozen 8-qubit schedule using $560$ million simulated training measurements per run achieves fidelity above $0.99$ in all $60$ held-out runs, with a mean fidelity of $0.996886$. 1080 circuit-noise cells and 6480 confidence-coverage rows are covered by fixed-circuit robustness validation without a locked soundness violation. We then address architectural fairness at $n=16$ using three new studies. In a 120-run exact-gradient multistart diagnostic, MERA has higher fidelity in 58/60 paired restarts and lower long-range error in 60/60, although no run met the prespecified stationarity criterion. Finally, a causal cone-complete, parameter matched local circuit achieves $2.62\times$ greater aggregate gate exposure yet loses all 30 paired comparisons in fidelity, long-range error, energy, and entropy.

</details>


### [114] [Interactions Protect Periodically Driven Time Crystals Against Dephasing but Destabilise Multipolar-Driven Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33574)
*Karthikeya Machiraju,Kaustav Bhowmick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了相互作用强度和配位数对周期驱动及随机多极驱动下时间晶体相干性的影响。发现强相互作用在周期驱动下保护时间晶体序，但在随机多极驱动下破坏序；配位数的作用超越了总相互作用强度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间晶体的相干性受退相位和驱动协议的影响机制尚不清楚。特别是相互作用强度、配位数（coordination）以及不同驱动序列（周期、随机多极、准周期）如何影响时间晶体序的稳定性，需要系统研究以理解其物理机制。

Method: 采用精确对角化方法研究了N=12和16个自旋的无序kicked Ising模型，考虑不完美π脉冲和单格点退相位。比较了周期驱动、n=0,1,2阶随机多极驱动和Thue-Morse序列；通过对比相同总相互作用强度zJ下的网格结构和环形结构，分离了耦合强度与配位数的影响；在三种相干时间定义下验证结果。

Result: 周期驱动下：相干时间约为1/γ标度，强耦合减少退相位作用的横向倾斜，网格结构的寿命是环形的2.15倍(N=12)和2.78倍(N=16)。随机多极驱动(n≥1)下：环形结构耦合加倍使相干时间缩短至0.54(n=1)、0.37(n=2)和0.44(Thue-Morse)，而随机符号(n=0)不受影响。无序扫描支持toggling-frame图像，表明相互作用破坏了脉冲误差的块消除。

Conclusion: 相互作用对时间晶体序的作用取决于驱动类型：在周期驱动下起保护作用，在随机多极驱动下起破坏作用。配位数的影响超越了总相互作用强度zJ的简单图像——相同zJ下，网格结构因配位数差异显著优于环形结构。这揭示了时间晶体稳定性的微观机制，对设计鲁棒的时间晶体系统具有指导意义。

Abstract: We show that stronger interactions protect periodically driven time-crystal order against dephasing but destabilise order under random multipolar drives, and that coordination matters beyond the total interaction strength. We studied a disordered kicked Ising model of $N=12$ and $16$ spins with imperfect $π$ pulses and per-site dephasing by exact diagonalisation, comparing a periodic drive, random multipolar drives of order $n=0,1,2$ and the Thue-Morse sequence, and separated coupling strength from coordination by comparing a grid with a ring at equal total interaction strength $zJ$. For the periodic drive the coherence time scales approximately as $1/γ$, and stronger coupling reduces the transverse tilt on which dephasing acts. For multipolar drives of order $n\ge1$, doubling the coupling on a ring shortens the coherence time to $0.54$ ($n=1$), $0.37$ ($n=2$) and $0.44$ (Thue-Morse) at $N=16$, while random signs ($n=0$) are unaffected. A disorder scan supports a toggling-frame picture in which interactions spoil the block cancellation of the pulse error. At equal $zJ$ the grid outlives the ring by $2.15$ ($N=12$) and $2.78$ ($N=16$) for the periodic drive despite an identical transverse tilt, a coordination effect beyond this picture. All ratios hold under three definitions of the coherence time.

</details>


### [115] [A technological capability scale for civilizations from the Kardashev and Barrow scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35237)
*Volkan Gurses*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的技术能力量表Ξ= K + B，通过文明每秒可执行的控制操作数量来排名。该量表结合了卡尔达肖夫指数K（能量规模）和巴罗效率指数B（每操作能耗），为评估文明技术水平提供了更全面的框架，并预测了极限技术状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的卡尔达肖夫量表仅关注文明的能量使用规模，忽略了能源使用效率这一关键维度。随着人类计算技术能效的快速提升（自1950年逻辑操作能耗下降了11.7个数量级），需要一个同时考虑能量规模和操作效率的综合指标来更准确地评估和预测文明的技术能力发展水平。

Method: 基于构造子理论（constructor theory），将控制操作定义为将物质的一个二进制自由度设置为指定值的过程，并分类为构造操作及其在信息媒介上的特殊情况（处理、存储和传输）。提出综合指数Ξ= K + B，其中K为卡尔达肖夫指数（衡量能量规模），B为巴罗效率指数（衡量每操作的能量消耗，以微波背景温度的热力学下限为基准）。通过分析热力学限制和黑洞物理，推导出技术发展的理论上限。

Result: 人类当前处于K = 0.73的水平，使用CMOS逻辑门在一次能源消耗方面达到B = -0.78。自1950年以来，每次逻辑操作的能耗已下降11.7个数量级，其中约5个数量级来自微型化技术。理论上的极限技术状态为Ξ= 7.58，该状态下将最大推测功率c^5/4G耗散到德西特空间中接近最大质量的不带电、非旋转黑洞中。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个更全面的文明技术能力评估框架，超越了传统卡尔达肖夫量表的局限性。通过同时考虑能量规模和操作效率，新量表Ξ不仅能更准确地描述当前人类文明的技术水平，还能基于物理学基本原理预测技术发展的理论极限。这为理解技术进步轨迹和评估先进文明的潜在能力提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a technological capability scale that ranks civilizations by the control operations they can perform per second. A control operation is defined as setting one binary degree of freedom of matter to a specified value, whatever its previous state. Building on constructor theory, we sort them into construction and its special cases on information media, namely processing, storage and transfer. The index $Ξ= K + B$ adds to the Kardashev index $K$ an efficiency index $B$ of energy per operation, measured from the thermal floor at the microwave background temperature. The Kardashev scale gives the ceiling for resets of thermally random bits with heat radiated into the present sky, and the Barrow scale gives the floor, which for such a reset is the thermal floor at the heat-rejection temperature at every level. Since 1950 the energy per logic operation has fallen 11.7 orders of magnitude, around 5 through miniaturization. Humanity, at $K = 0.73$, computes with CMOS logic gates at $B = -0.78$ in primary energy. We describe the ultimate technology, at $Ξ= 7.58$, which would dissipate the conjectured largest power $c^5/4G$ into an uncharged, non-rotating black hole near its largest mass in de Sitter space.

</details>


### [116] [Simultaneously Query-Optimal Quantum Linear-System Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33686)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子线性系统算法中矩阵查询复杂度和状态制备查询复杂度能否同时达到最优的开放性问题，证明了单个算法可以同时实现 O(κlog(1/ε)) 次矩阵查询和 O(κ/s) 次状态制备查询，并给出了匹配的下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统算法分别通过块编码预言机 U_A 访问系数矩阵和通过状态制备预言机 U_b 访问右端项。虽然这两个预言机的最优查询复杂度已分别已知（O(κlog(1/ε)) 次 U_A 查询和 O(κ/s) 次 U_b 查询），但是否存在单个算法能同时达到这两个界一直是未解决的开放性问题。解决这一问题对于理解量子线性系统算法的基本复杂度限制具有重要意义。

Method: 研究者提出了一种将状态制备与精度细化分离的量子线性系统算法。该算法在给定解范数尺度 s=α‖A^(-1)b‖ 的常数因子估计的前提下工作，使得只有矩阵查询次数依赖于目标精度 ε，而状态制备查询次数仅依赖于条件数 κ 和解的范数参数 s。此外，研究者还构造了匹配的同实例最坏情况下界证明：对于每个量子线性系统算法和每个解范数尺度，都存在一个厄米实例使得该算法同时需要 Ω(κlog(1/ε)) 次矩阵查询和 Ω(κ/s) 次状态制备查询。

Result: 提出的算法成功实现了同时达到两个最优查询复杂度界：O(κlog(1/ε)) 次矩阵预言机 U_A 查询和 O(κ/s) 次状态制备预言机 U_b 查询，从而将归一化解制备到误差 ε 以内。同时证明了匹配的下界 Ω(κlog(1/ε)) 和 Ω(κ/s)，表明该算法在两个查询复杂度上都是渐近最优的。这一结果肯定地回答了两种预言机复杂度能否同时优化的问题。

Conclusion: 本文确定性地解决了量子线性系统算法中的一个基本开放性问题：两种预言机的查询复杂度可以被同时优化，并且所得到的界是联合最优的。通过将状态制备与精度细化分离的创新设计，以及严格的下界证明，研究证明了 O(κlog(1/ε)) 矩阵查询和 O(κ/s) 状态制备查询的组合是紧致的最优复杂度。这一结果为量子线性系统算法的理论基础提供了完整的刻画，明确了该问题的计算复杂度边界。

Abstract: Quantum linear-system algorithms access the coefficient matrix through a block-encoding oracle $U_A$ and the right-hand side through a state-preparation oracle $U_b$. The optimal query complexities of these two oracles are known separately: $O(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries to $U_A$ and $O(κ/s)$ queries to $U_b$, where $s=α\|A^{-1}b\|$. Whether a single algorithm can achieve both bounds simultaneously has remained open.
  We resolve this question affirmatively. Given a constant-factor estimate of $s$, we give a quantum linear-system algorithm that prepares the normalized solution to error $\varepsilon$ using simultaneously $ O\left(κ\log\frac1\varepsilon\right)$ queries to $U_A$ and $O\left(κ/{s}\right)$ queries to $U_b$. The algorithm separates state preparation from precision refinement, so only the matrix-query count depends on the target accuracy.
  We also prove a matching same-instance worst-case lower bound: for every quantum linear-system algorithm and every solution-norm scale, there exists a single Hermitian instance on which that algorithm requires both $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix queries and $Ω(κ/s)$ state-preparation queries. Thus the two oracle complexities can be optimized simultaneously, and the resulting bounds are jointly optimal.

</details>


### [117] [On Embedding Design in Quantum Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33700)
*Ban Q. Tran,Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Susan Mengel,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了量子物理信息神经网络(QPINNs)的统一嵌入框架,开发了两种新型嵌入方法(LQNN-TE-QPINN和AdaFreq-QPINN),通过改进坐标到量子态的映射方式,显著提升了求解偏微分方程的精度和参数效率,证明了嵌入设计对QPINNs性能的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理信息神经网络通过训练参数化量子电路来求解偏微分方程,但将坐标映射到量子态的嵌入机制的作用尚未被充分理解。现有方法在特征表示和参数效率方面存在局限性,需要系统性地研究嵌入设计如何影响QPINNs的近似能力和优化行为,以提升量子算法在科学计算中的实用性。

Method: 研究建立了统一的嵌入框架,将嵌入表述为塑造变分电路特征表示的泛函变换,并在此框架下提出两种新方法:1)线性量子神经网络可训练嵌入QPINN(LQNN-TE-QPINN),使用辅助量子电路生成数据依赖特征并将其加到输入坐标上(而非相乘);2)自适应频率QPINN(AdaFreq-QPINN),将arccos-Chebyshev编码的频率缩放因子提升为可训练参数。在一维和二维Burgers方程上对比评估了直接编码、固定解析频率编码和替代混合架构。

Result: 在一维问题中,LQNN-TE-QPINN使用720个可训练参数实现0.0916的相对L2误差,优于直接编码QPINN(0.4501)和参数量约为其11倍的局部自适应经典物理信息神经网络(0.1233);AdaFreq-QPINN仅增加4个参数即在解析嵌入方法中达到最低误差。在二维问题中,LQNN-TE-QPINN获得最低训练目标值,但其求解误差与数据重上传QPINN未呈现明显分离。在模拟硬件噪声下,LQNN-TE-QPINN保持最低绝对导数误差,但相对其无噪声基线退化最明显。

Conclusion: 研究证明嵌入设计对量子物理信息神经网络的近似和优化行为具有实质性影响。提出的LQNN-TE-QPINN和AdaFreq-QPINN方法在参数效率和求解精度上展现出显著优势,为量子机器学习求解偏微分方程提供了新的设计范式。统一嵌入框架为后续研究量子特征映射与变分量子算法性能的关系奠定了理论基础,但噪声鲁棒性仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) solve partial differential equations (PDEs) by training parameterized quantum circuits against physics-based residuals, yet the role of the embedding that maps coordinates into quantum states remains insufficiently understood. In this research, we introduce a unified embedding framework that formulates embedding as a functional transformation shaping the feature representation available to the variational circuit, and we propose two embeddings within it. The Linear Quantum Neural Network Trainable Embedding QPINN (LQNN-TE-QPINN) generates data-dependent features with an auxiliary quantum circuit and adds them to the input coordinates, instead of multiplying them as in our previous formulation. The adaptive-frequency QPINN (AdaFreq-QPINN) promotes the frequency-scaling factors of an arccos-Chebyshev encoding to trainable parameters. We evaluate both against direct and fixed analytical frequency encodings and alternative hybrid architectures on one- and two-dimensional Burgers equations. In one dimension, LQNN-TE-QPINN achieves a relative $L_2$ error of 0.0916 with 720 trainable parameters, compared with 0.4501 for direct-encoding QPINN and 0.1233 for a locally adaptive classical physics-informed neural network with approximately eleven times more parameters, while AdaFreq-QPINN attains the lowest error among the analytical embeddings with only four additional parameters. In two dimensions, LQNN-TE-QPINN attains the lowest training objective, although its solution error is not clearly separated from that of a data re-uploading QPINN. Under simulated hardware noise, LQNN-TE-QPINN retains the lowest absolute derivative error while degrading most relative to its noiseless baseline. These results demonstrate that embedding design substantially influences the approximation and optimization behavior of QPINNs.

</details>


### [118] [Autonomous phase discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33802)
*Shiyu Zhou,Yuxuan Zhang,Sebastian Wetzel,Roger Melko,Xiu-Zhe Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合可微分编程和无监督学习的全自主系统，用于量子相的自主发现。该系统通过自适应轨迹评估基态数据，无需预设参数网格或相标签，在有限计算预算下能比随机采样发现更多量子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子物质相的理解依赖物理直觉和对称性、拓扑等数学工具，但这些方法无法提供通用的方式来探索组织原理未知的哈密顿量空间。需要一种不依赖先验知识、无需相标签或预设目标相的自主探索方法来发现新的量子相。

Method: 构建了一个全自主系统，结合了可微分编程和无监督学习技术。系统沿自适应轨迹而非预定参数网格评估基态数据，采用梯度驱动的探索策略。使用三种不同的求解器进行实现，并在相同基态评估预算下与随机采样方法进行基准测试比较。

Result: 在包含多达200个不同相的广义cluster链上，该系统在相同计算预算下比随机采样多发现了25个相，并且仅用随机采样三十分之一的预算即可达到相同效果。在50参数Chern绝缘体上，系统发现了简单谐波构造无法获得的区域(该体系的逆问题仍未解决),同时恢复了采样方法找到的所有区域。

Conclusion: 研究确立了自主的、梯度驱动的哈密顿量空间探索作为发现量子相的实用路径，该方法无需相标签或预设目标相。这为探索组织原理未知的量子系统提供了新的有效工具，展示了机器学习与量子物理结合在相发现中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Understanding quantum phases of matter has long relied on physicists' intuition and mathematical tools such as symmetry and topology. Remarkably successful as these approaches have been, they provide no universal way to explore a Hamiltonian space whose organizing principle is not known in advance. In this work, we introduce a fully autonomous system combining differentiable programming and unsupervised learning for quantum phase discovery. The search evaluates ground-state data along an adaptive trajectory rather than on a predetermined parameter grid. We demonstrate the system with three different solvers and benchmark it against random sampling at equal ground-state-evaluation budgets. On a generalized cluster chain hosting up to $200$ distinct phases, the search finds up to $25$ more phases at the same budget, and matches random sampling given thirty times its budget. On a $50$-parameter Chern insulator, it reaches sectors not obtained by the simple harmonic constructions considered here, in a family whose inverse problem remains open, while recovering all sectors found by sampling. Our results establish autonomous, gradient-driven exploration of Hamiltonian space as a practical route to discovering quantum phases without phase labels or a prescribed target phase.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum data processing equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33815)
*Tai-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过复数条带上的边界和表面Poisson积分表示，将量子数据处理不等式提升为精确等式，将相对熵损失分解为Petz恢复分量和相对模动力学失配项，并建立了具有平方根最优缩放的恢复界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子数据处理不等式只给出了量子相对熵在量子信道下单调非增的定性结果，缺乏对相对熵损失的精确量化和物理机制的深入理解。现有的恢复界在小特征值依赖上存在逆幂次缩放问题，需要改进。

Method: 利用复数条带上的边界和表面Poisson积分表示技术，将相对熵损失表示为精确等式；将损失分解为操作性Petz恢复分量和边界项（测量相对模动力学失配）；进一步将损失表示为原始态与平均Petz恢复态之间的测量相对熵加上非负变分余项；对Belavkin-Staszewski损失进行类似的积分表示和分解；在任意给定模参数下建立恢复界，包括标准未旋转Petz映射。

Result: 成功将量子数据处理不等式提升为精确等式，识别出相对熵损失的两个独立非负物理机制；建立了具有相对熵损失平方根最优缩放的恢复界；预因子依赖于不同特征值计数或对数谱量，显著改进了先前界中对小特征值的逆幂次依赖；对BS损失获得了类似的分解结构，表示为与平均重构的负对数Uhlmann保真度加上非负变分余项。

Conclusion: 本研究通过Poisson积分表示将量子相对熵的单调性从不等式精确化为等式，揭示了相对熵损失的深层物理机制（Petz恢复和模动力学失配），并建立了具有平方根最优缩放和改进预因子依赖的恢复界，为量子信息理论中的态恢复问题提供了更精细的数学工具和物理洞察。

Abstract: The quantum data processing inequality asserts that quantum relative entropy is monotonically non-increasing under quantum channels. Here, we promote this fundamental inequality to an exact equality via boundary and surface Poisson integral representations across a complex strip. The relative-entropy loss is resolved into two independent, nonnegative physical mechanisms: an operational Petz recovery component governing state reconstructibility, and a boundary term measuring the mismatch of relative modular dynamics. We further express the loss as the measured relative entropy between the original state and its averaged Petz recovery state plus a nonnegative variational remainder. For the Belavkin--Staszewski (BS) loss, analogous integral representations yield a decomposition into negative log Uhlmann fidelity with an averaged reconstruction plus a nonnegative variational remainder. Furthermore, we establish recovery bounds at any prescribed modular parameter, including the canonical unrotated Petz map, with optimal square-root scaling in the relative entropy loss. The prefactors depend on distinct-eigenvalue counts or logarithmic spectral quantities, improving upon the inverse-power dependence on small eigenvalues in previous bounds.

</details>


### [120] [Dynamic Sculpting of Photon Statistics in a Raman-Coupled Cavity QED System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33837)
*Amir Rahmani,Alireza Dehghani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过量子干涉动态调控光子统计特性的方案，利用拉曼耦合腔-原子系统和参数放大，通过周期性调制实现光子聚束和反聚束之间的主动切换，在连续波操作下实现时间门控的光子反聚束效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稳态光子统计特性已被广泛研究，但动态调控这些统计特性在时间维度上仍然难以实现。光子统计特性的主动调控对于经典和量子光子学的诸多应用至关重要，因此需要开发一种能够在聚束和反聚束态之间灵活切换的机制。

Method: 采用具有参数放大的拉曼耦合腔-原子系统。通过时间调制中间态失谐来加深光子阻塞或增强光子聚束；通过同时调制腔失谐和中间态失谐产生静态条件或单独调制无法实现的光子反聚束。利用周期性调制使反聚束效应在固定时间间隔重复出现，调制频率决定重复率。该机制基于拉曼辅助量子干涉原理。

Result: 成功实现了光子统计特性在聚束和反聚束态之间的动态调控。同时调制腔失谐和中间态失谐能够产生静态条件下无法获得的光子反聚束效应。在连续波操作条件下实现了时间门控的光子反聚束，其重复率由调制频率确定。

Conclusion: 展示了一种基于拉曼辅助量子干涉的通用机制，可用于动态工程化光子关联特性。该方案为在时间维度上主动操控光子统计提供了可行途径，通过周期性调制实现了静态条件下无法达到的光子反聚束效应，为量子光学和光子学应用开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Controlling photon statistics is pivotal to many applications in classical and quantum photonics. While steady-state photon statistics have been widely studied, dynamically manipulating these statistical properties in time remains elusive. Herein, we propose a scheme to actively control photon statistics between bunching and antibunching regimes through quantum interference. Our approach relies on a Raman-coupled cavity-atom system with parametric amplification, where time modulating the detuning of the intermediate state allows us to either deepen the photon blockade or enhance photon bunching. Simultaneous modulation of the cavity and intermediate-state detunings produces antibunching that cannot be achieved under static conditions or by modulating either detuning alone. Because the modulation is periodic, the antibunching reappears at regular time intervals, enabling time-gated photon antibunching under continuous-wave operation at a repetition rate determined by the modulation frequency. These results show a versatile mechanism based on Raman-assisted quantum interference for dynamically engineering photon correlations.

</details>


### [121] [Nonlinear engineering of Hong-Ou-Mandel interference with structured light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33862)
*Simon Darveau,Bienvenu Ndagano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了泵浦光场的宇称对Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉仪中光子对干涉行为的影响,通过使用厄米-高斯和拉盖尔-高斯结构化泵浦模式,实现了从相长干涉到相消干涉的转变调控,并展示了基于轨道角动量的Hong-Ou-Mandel增强旋转传感的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉中,光子对的对称性通常通过对光子对施加线性变换和投影来实现。本研究旨在探索另一种途径——通过调控泵浦光场本身来工程化光子对的对称性,特别是研究泵浦光场的宇称如何影响干涉转变的形状和频率特性,以开发更高灵敏度的量子传感技术。

Method: 研究采用理论与实验相结合的方法。具体使用旋转的结构化泵浦模式,分别编码在厄米-高斯(Hermite-Gaussian)基和拉盖尔-高斯(Laguerre-Gaussian)基中。通过自发参量下转换过程产生光子对,在Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉仪中测量这些光子对的聚束或反聚束行为,分析从相长干涉到相消干涉的转变特性。

Result: 在厄米-高斯基编码的情况下,干涉转变呈现出该模式家族特有的丰富结构,并且随着模式阶数的增加,传感灵敏度得到增强。在拉盖尔-高斯基编码的情况下,角分辨率的整数倍增强与轨道角动量含量成正比,类似于NOON态的表现,实现了角分辨率的显著提升。

Conclusion: 泵浦光场的宇称是调控Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉行为的有效手段。通过精心设计结构化泵浦模式,可以实现干涉转变特性的定制化控制。特别是利用轨道角动量编码的拉盖尔-高斯泵浦模式,能够实现角分辨率的整数倍增强,为开发基于Hong-Ou-Mandel效应的高灵敏度旋转传感器开辟了新的可能性,在量子计量学和精密测量领域具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: A pair of photons entering a Hong-Ou-Mandel interferometer will bunch or anti-bunch depending on the symmetry of their state in the measured degree of freedom, resulting in constructive or destructive interference. For photon pairs generated through spontaneous parametric down-conversion, this symmetry can be engineered through linear transformations and projections applied to the photon pairs, or by tailoring the pump field. Here, we adopt the latter approach to demonstrate that the pump's parity affects the shape and frequency of the transition from constructive to destructive interference. We demonstrate this concept theoretically and experimentally using a rotating structured pump mode encoded in the Hermite-Gaussian and Laguerre-Gaussian bases. In the former case, we show that the transition exhibits a rich structure unique to the mode family, resulting in enhanced sensitivity as the mode order increases. In the latter case, we demonstrate, as with NOON states, an integer enhancement in angular resolution by a factor proportional to the orbital angular momentum content, thereby opening the possibility of Hong-Ou-Mandel-enhanced rotation sensing.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum-Computing Self-Consistent Kohn-Sham DFT: Plane-Wave-Orthonormalized Orbitals with a Dual-Basis Quantum Eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33934)
*Lazaro Calderin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子计算机的第一量子化、全电子、全势Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论方法,采用经典优化的量子电路本征求解器处理自洽场特征问题,并引入平面波正交化轨道(PWOO)基组,在H₈链体系中验证了该方法的准确性,并在IonQ Forte-1量子计算机上实现了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子化学计算在经典计算机上面临计算资源瓶颈,特别是处理大规模电子结构问题时。虽然量子计算为解决这类问题提供了新途径,但现有量子算法在实际应用中仍存在量子比特需求高、电路深度大等挑战。因此,需要开发一种高效的量子算法,能够在有限的量子资源下实现精确的密度泛函理论计算,并适应当前噪声中等规模量子(NISQ)设备的限制。

Method: 本文提出的方法包括以下关键技术:(1)基于经典优化的量子电路本征求解器处理任意正交基下的自洽场(SCF)特征问题;(2)设计了平面波正交化轨道(PWOO)基组,将平面波与原子轨道(AOs)结合并相互正交化;(3)采用双基组形式的求解策略:在全基组bootstrap步骤后,在K个函数的收缩基组(需要⌈log₂K⌉个量子比特)中求解每个SCF步,当残差超过阈值时在全基组中更新;(4)通过状态矢量模拟和实际量子硬件(IonQ Forte-1)验证算法性能,测试了噪声对收敛性的影响。

Result: 在H₈链体系(SVWN泛函,K=8)的测试中:(1)状态矢量模拟的SCF计算结果与经典计算在32768个平面波和cc-pVTZ以下的PWOO基组中误差在0.02 mHa以内;(2)基组收缩对PWOO能量的改变最多为5.7 μHa;(3)在模拟散粒噪声条件下,STO-3G PWOO SCF能够在无噪声不动点附近收敛;(4)在IonQ Forte-1量子计算机上,三量子比特寄存器读出的PWOO-AO能量差在一个标准差范围内重现了无噪声结果,验证了方法在实际量子硬件上的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种适用于量子计算机的全电子密度泛函理论方法,通过PWOO基组和双基组收缩策略有效降低了量子比特需求。状态矢量模拟和实际量子硬件实验均验证了该方法的准确性和实用性。该方法为在近期量子设备上进行实际化学体系的第一性原理计算提供了可行路径,展示了量子计算在量子化学领域应用的潜力,并为未来开发更大规模、更复杂体系的量子算法奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a first-quantization, all-electron, full-potential Kohn-Sham density functional theory method for quantum computers, based on a classically optimized quantum-circuit eigensolver for the self-consistent field (SCF) eigenproblems in any orthonormal basis. The plane-wave-orthonormalized-orbital (PWOO) basis combines plane waves with atomic orbitals (AOs) orthonormalized against them and one another. The eigensolver has a dual-basis form: after full-basis bootstrap steps, it solves each SCF step in a contracted basis of $K$ functions ($\lceil\log_2 K\rceil$ qubits), updated in the full basis when the residual outside it exceeds a threshold. For an H$_8$ chain (SVWN, $K=8$), the state-vector-emulated SCF agrees with classical calculations within 0.02 mHa for 32768 plane waves and PWOO bases up to cc-pVTZ; contraction changes these PWOO energies by at most 5.7 $μ$Ha. With emulated shot noise, the STO-3G PWOO SCF converges near the noise-free fixed point. On IonQ Forte-1, three-qubit-register readout reproduces the noise-free PWOO-AO energy difference within one standard deviation.

</details>


### [123] [Hardness of Approximating Quantum Code Distance Beyond $\sqrt{N}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33956)
*Upendra Kapshikar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子码最小距离近似问题的计算复杂度,证明了该问题在经典和量子情况下的NP困难性,并建立了精细复杂度下界,表明在标准复杂度假设下不存在高效近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典纠错码的最小距离问题已被证明是NP困难的,但对于量子稳定子码,现有研究仅达到O(√N)的加性近似间隙,未能达到与经典情况相同的线性间隙。需要填补这一理论空白,并从精细复杂度角度深入理解该问题的计算难度边界。

Method: 采用复杂度理论归约方法,通过构造从NP问题到稳定子码最小距离问题的归约,建立计算困难性证明。在精细复杂度分析中,基于强指数时间假设(SETH)和间隙指数时间假设(Gap-ETH)建立条件下界,分析不同参数regime下算法时间复杂度的本质限制。

Result: 证明了三个主要结果:(1)除非NP⊆coRP,否则不存在随机算法能在加性αN(α>0为常数)误差内近似最小距离;(2)对任意ε>0,除非SETH不成立,否则不存在2^((1-ε)κ)·poly(N)时间的随机算法能精确计算具有κ个逻辑量子比特的码的最小距离;(3)在码长、逻辑量子比特数和间隙同时为线性的实例上,除非非一致Gap-ETH不成立,否则不存在2^(o(N))时间算法能在线性加性间隙内近似最小距离。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了量子稳定子码最小距离近似问题的复杂度刻画,将近似困难性从O(√N)提升至线性Ω(N),与经典情况一致。同时首次建立了该问题的精细复杂度理论,揭示了在标准复杂度假设下,精确计算和近似算法都面临指数级时间障碍,为量子纠错码设计和分析的计算限制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximating the minimum distance of a stabilizer code. Classically, the minimum distance problem is NP-hard to approximate even to within an additive gap linear in the block length. For quantum codes, however, the reductions of Kapshikar and Kundu and of Grigorescu, Jha and Samperton reach only an additive $O(\sqrt{N})$ gap in the block length $N$. We close this gap and, in a separate direction, initiate the fine-grained study of the problem.
  We show that no randomized algorithm approximates the distance to within an additive $αN$, for a constant $α> 0$, unless $\mathsf{NP} \subseteq \mathsf{coRP}$. In the fine-grained setting, for every $\varepsilon > 0$, no randomized $2^{(1-\varepsilon)κ}\,\mathrm{poly}(N)$-time algorithm computes the distance of a code with $κ$ logical qubits unless SETH falls; and no randomized $2^{o(N)}$-time algorithm approximates the distance to within a linear additive gap, on instances where block length, number of logical qubits, and gap are simultaneously linear, unless non-uniform Gap-ETH falls.

</details>


### [124] [Swapping Quantum Annealing Errors into a Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33975)
*Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"腔量子退火"方法，通过在退火过程中耦合腔模将错误转换为光子，使退火时间降至原来的平方根量级，并通过光子计数实现结果认证，显著提升量子退火的保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子退火在小能隙处会显著减速，且产生的错误无法被检测。这限制了量子退火作为量子计算范式的实用性和可靠性，需要一种方法来加速退火过程并实现错误检测。

Method: 提出腔量子退火（cavity quantum annealing）方案：在退火过程中期耦合一个频率为O(1)量级（远大于能隙Δ）的腔模，利用腔模与量子系统的相互作用将错误态交换到光子态中。通过光子计数可以认证计算结果——高光子数运行对应高保真度的基态。该方法还可逆向操作，通过预加载光子制备激发态。

Result: 在p-自旋模型中，腔量子退火将退火时间降低至无腔情况的近平方根量级。高光子数运行（产率为O(1)量级）能够更快达到99.99%以上的基态保真度，且这一优势随系统规模呈指数级增长，比传统方法快几个数量级。

Conclusion: 腔模可以有效增强基于量子比特的计算性能。腔量子退火不仅大幅缩短了退火时间，还通过光子计数实现了结果认证和错误检测，为通过量子退火实现高保真量子态制备开辟了新途径，展示了腔-量子比特混合系统在量子计算中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising form of quantum computation, but it slows down at a small energy gap $Δ$ during the anneal, and the errors it makes there remain undetected. We introduce cavity quantum annealing, in which a cavity mode of frequency $O(1)\ggΔ$, coupled mid-schedule, swaps these errors into photons. In the $p$-spin model, this reduces the annealing time to nearly the square root of that required without the cavity. Moreover, counting the photons certifies the result: high-photon runs, with an $O(1)$ yield, reach a ground-state fidelity above $99.99\%$ orders of magnitude sooner, a gain that grows exponentially with system size. A pre-loaded photon instead runs the swap in reverse, preparing an excited state. More broadly, our work shows that cavities can enhance computations performed with qubits, paving the way toward high-fidelity quantum state preparation by quantum annealing.

</details>


### [125] [QBX: A Compiler for 2-local Qubit Hamiltonian Simulation on Quantum Chiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33997)
*Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Zhihao Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了QBX，这是首个专为量子芯片架构设计的2-局域量子比特哈密顿量模拟编译器，通过分层方法和高速通道机制显著降低跨芯片通信成本，在电路深度和操作数量上优于现有通用和专用编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用量子编译器在门级操作，无法利用哈密顿量模拟的高层语义进行优化；而现有的哈密顿量模拟专用编译器针对单片架构设计，可扩展性有限且无法适应芯片架构的异构性，导致电路输出次优。需要一个专门针对量子芯片架构的2-局域哈密顿量模拟编译器。

Method: QBX采用可扩展的分层方法来减少跨芯片通信的数量和距离；集成高速通道（highway）机制实现面向芯片的高效长程量子比特通信，降低跨芯片交互成本；将2-局域泡利串高效聚合为多目标受控门，利用高速通道进行深度优化。

Result: 评估结果显示，QBX在电路深度和操作数量方面明显优于通用编译器和专用编译器，同时能够扩展到更大规模的2-局域哈密顿量模拟任务。

Conclusion: QBX通过针对量子芯片架构的专门设计，成功解决了现有编译器在2-局域哈密顿量模拟任务中的可扩展性和优化不足问题，为量子芯片架构上的哈密顿量模拟提供了高效的编译解决方案。

Abstract: 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation, a fundamental task in quantum computing, is widely applied in various applications. This paper presents QBX, the first quantum compiler designed for 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation on quantum chiplet architectures. Existing general-purpose quantum compilers for chiplet architectures target quantum programs at the gate level and miss optimizations requiring high-level semantics of Hamiltonian simulation. Conversely, existing domain-specific compilers for Hamiltonian simulation are designed for monolithic architectures, and their limited scalability and inability to adapt to the heterogeneity of chiplet architectures lead to sub-optimal circuit outputs. QBX implements a scalable hierarchical approach to reduce both the amount and distance of cross-chiplet communications. It integrates the highway mechanism, a chiplet-oriented solution for efficient long-range qubit communication, to diminish cross-chiplet interaction costs. Furthermore, QBX efficiently aggregates 2-local Pauli strings into multi-target controlled gates, harnessing highways for deep optimization. Our evaluations show that QBX markedly outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific compilers in circuit depth and operation count, while scaling to much larger 2-local Hamiltonian simulations.

</details>


### [126] [Nakayama's reduction of quantum topos and Bayesian quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34200)
*Atsuhide Mori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解释了Nakayama在Döring-Isham拓扑斯量子理论中对上下文限制的统一方法,阐述了这种统一如何支持贝叶斯概率观点,并将其应用于量子计算中预测理论适用性的局部转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Döring-Isham拓扑斯量子理论框架中,需要对上下文进行限制并定义概率。Nakayama发现了一种通过单一拓扑选择来统一这两个方面的方法。本研究旨在阐明这种统一如何为贝叶斯概率观点提供支持,即概率随着数据获取而变化,并探索其在量子计算中的应用价值。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法,基于Nakayama在拓扑斯量子理论中的工作,通过拓扑学框架统一上下文限制和概率定义。将概率的变化描述为预测理论适用性的局部转移(local shift),并将这一理论框架应用于量子计算场景,解释贝叶斯更新过程在量子系统中的体现。

Result: 研究展示了Nakayama的拓扑统一方法如何自然地支持贝叶斯概率观点,即概率随观测数据获取而动态更新。通过将概率变化解释为预测理论适用性的局部转移,成功地将这一理论框架应用于量子计算,为理解量子系统中的概率演化提供了新的数学工具和概念框架。

Conclusion: Nakayama通过单一拓扑选择统一上下文限制与概率定义的方法,为拓扑斯量子理论提供了坚实的数学基础,并且这种统一天然地支持贝叶斯概率观。通过将概率更新理解为预测理论适用性的局部转移,该框架可以有效应用于量子计算,为量子信息处理中的概率推理提供了新的理论视角和数学工具。

Abstract: Nakayama found the way to limit the contexts in the Döring-Isham topos quantum theory and unified such limitation and the definition of probability as a single choice of topology. We explain how this unification supports the Bayesian perspective where the probability changes as data is obtained. We describe this change as a local shift of the applicability of a predictive theory and apply it to quantum computing.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum memory in an effective $Λ$-type system using dynamic detuning control of a superconducting qubit pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34201)
*Hsin Chang,Kai-I Chu,Ite A. Yu,Yen-Hsiang Lin,Yung-Fu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两个可调超导量子比特的量子光存储方案,通过动态控制量子比特失谐实现量子场的存储和按需读取,为可扩展超导量子网络提供了最小化的可调量子存储架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子网络需要高效的量子存储器来存储和检索量子光场。超导量子比特系统具有可调控性和可扩展性优势,但需要设计合适的架构来实现长寿命的量子态存储。本研究旨在利用超辐射态和亚辐射态之间的耦合,构建一个最小化且可调的量子存储系统,为可扩展的超导量子网络奠定基础。

Method: 研究采用理论分析方法,构建了由一对可调超导量子比特组成的有效Λ型三能级系统。两个量子比特耦合到半无限传输线,相距半个波长。通过调节量子比特之间的频率失谐,实现超辐射激发态与亚辐射亚稳态之间的直接耦合。系统展现可控的频谱响应和慢光行为。通过动态控制失谐参数,入射量子场被映射到亚辐射集体态上,以长寿命激发的形式存储,并可按需检索。

Result: 理论分析表明,该系统通过调节量子比特失谐可以实现可控的频谱响应和慢光效应。动态控制失谐使得入射量子场能够有效映射到亚辐射集体态,实现长时间存储,并能按需读取。该方案验证了使用最小化配置(仅两个量子比特)实现量子存储的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个基于可调超导量子比特对的最小化量子存储架构,为可扩展的超导量子网络提供了有效的量子存储解决方案。该系统具有结构简单、可调控性强、存储寿命长等优点,为超导量子信息处理和量子网络的实际应用提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum-light storage in an effective $Λ$-type system formed by a pair of tunable superconducting qubits coupled to a semi-infinite transmission line and separated by half a wavelength. The frequency detuning between the qubits directly couples the superradiant excited state and the subradiant metastable state. By adjusting the qubit detuning, the system exhibits a controllable spectral response accompanied by slow-light behavior. Dynamically controlling the detuning enables an incident quantum field to be mapped onto the subradiant collective state, stored as a long-lived excitation, and subsequently retrieved on demand. Our results establish the proposed system as a minimal and tunable quantum-memory architecture for scalable superconducting quantum networks.

</details>


### [128] [Monotonicity of the Rényi channel capacity under non-signaling assisted channel simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34213)
*Tina Shariat,Matt Hoogsteder-Riera,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非信号关联(non-signaling correlations)无法提升经典信道在任何阶数下的Rényi容量,这一结果对随机编码指数、球填充指数和强逆定理指数都有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非信号盒(non-signaling boxes)构成了比共享随机性辅助的编码器-解码器对更大的类别,传统基于独立预处理和后处理的论证方法不再适用,同时非信号盒的约束在盒中是线性的,而Rényi容量对信道的依赖是非线性的。这种线性与非线性之间的不匹配需要新的数学工具来解决,以确定非信号关联是否能够提升经典信道的Rényi容量。

Method: 采用基于Legendre变换的变分方法,将容量重新表述为一族仿射泛函的极值问题,在这个新表述中信道以线性方式出现。结合针对Shannon容量和端点阶数的独立论证,系统地分析从零阶到Shannon容量再到无穷阶的所有Rényi容量阶数。

Result: 证明了在所有阶数(从0阶到Shannon容量再到∞阶)上,非信号盒都无法提升Rényi容量。进一步推导出非信号模拟不能增加容量以下的随机编码指数或球填充指数,也不能降低容量以上的强逆定理指数。

Conclusion: 本文通过创新的变分方法彻底解决了非信号关联与经典信道Rényi容量的关系问题,证明非信号资源在信道容量问题中不提供额外优势。这一结果不仅适用于所有阶数的Rényi容量,还对信道编码的误差指数理论产生直接影响,表明非信号关联在经典通信的基本限制方面不改变已知的容量边界。

Abstract: We ask whether non-signaling correlations shared between the sender and receiver can increase the Rényi capacity of a classical channel. Non-signaling boxes form a strictly larger class than that of encoder and decoder pairs assisted by shared randomness, so the usual argument based on independent preprocessing and postprocessing is insufficient, and the constraints defining such boxes are linear in the box, while the Rényi capacity depends on the channel nonlinearly. We resolve this mismatch with a variational approach based on the Legendre transform, which recasts the capacity as an extremum over a family of affine functionals in which the channel appears linearly. Combining this method with separate arguments for the Shannon capacity and endpoint orders, we prove that no non-signaling box can raise the Rényi capacity at any order, from order zero through the Shannon capacity to the limiting order at infinity. Consequently, non-signaling simulation cannot increase the random-coding or sphere-packing exponent below capacity, or decrease the strong-converse exponent above capacity.

</details>


### [129] [A threshold for maximal Schmidt number from spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34255)
*Junhyeong An,Soojoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双分系统量子态的Schmidt数与谱性质之间的关系,通过推导(d-1)-块正算子的谱界,证明了特定谱条件可保证Schmidt数不达到最大值,并给出了涉及全局幺正变换下约化判据与Schmidt数关系的应用结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schmidt数是量子纠缠维度的重要量化指标,但判定其是否达到最大值通常较为困难。现有研究缺乏仅基于量子态谱特性就能判断Schmidt数非最大性的简洁充分条件。此外,约化判据与Schmidt数在全局幺正变换下的关系尚未完全阐明,需要建立更系统的理论框架来刻画这些量子纠缠特性之间的联系。

Method: 文章采用算子理论和谱分析方法:(1)针对维度为d×d的双分系统,推导(d-1)-块正算子的谱界条件;(2)定义集合LS_p(最大特征值不超过p个最小特征值之和的态)和ASN_{d-1}(在所有全局幺正变换下Schmidt数至多为d-1的态),证明当p≤⌊d²/2⌋时LS_p⊆ASN_{d-1};(3)建立仅涉及最大特征值的简洁充分条件;(4)分析满足任意全局幺正变换下约化判据的态的Schmidt数性质;(5)构造反例验证全局幺正要求的必要性。

Result: 主要结果包括:(1)证明了LS_p⊆ASN_{d-1}对p≤⌊d²/2⌋成立,提供了基于谱条件判断Schmidt数非最大性的理论依据;(2)导出了仅依赖最大特征值的简洁充分条件;(3)证明在任意维度下,满足全局幺正变换下约化判据的态属于ASN_{d-1},该结果推广了绝对正部分转置态属于ASN_{d-1}的已知结论;(4)对每个d≥3,构造了在两个子系统上都满足约化判据但具有最大Schmidt数的态,证明全局幺正要求不可或缺。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子态谱性质与Schmidt数之间的明确联系,证明特定的谱条件可保证Schmidt数在全局幺正变换下保持非最大性。研究揭示了约化判据与Schmidt数的深层关系,表明在全局幺正变换下满足约化判据的态必然具有非最大Schmidt数,同时通过反例证明全局幺正要求是本质性的。这些结果为量子纠缠维度的判定提供了新的谱分析工具,深化了对双分系统纠缠结构的理解。

Abstract: The Schmidt number quantifies the dimensionality of entanglement in bipartite quantum states. We investigate when the spectrum of a state on $\mathbb{C}^d \otimes \mathbb{C}^d$ alone guarantees that its Schmidt number is not maximal. By deriving spectral bounds for $(d-1)$-block positive operators, we prove that $\mathrm{LS}_p\subseteq \mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ for $p\leq \lfloor d^2/2\rfloor$, where $\mathrm{LS}_p$ denotes the set of states whose largest eigenvalue does not exceed the sum of their $p$ smallest eigenvalues, and $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ denotes the set of states whose Schmidt number remains at most $d-1$ under all global unitaries. This also yields a simple sufficient condition involving only the largest eigenvalue. As an application, we show that states satisfying the reduction criterion under arbitrary global unitaries belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ in every dimension. This subsumes the earlier result that absolutely positive partial transpose states belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$. The global unitary requirement is nevertheless essential: for every $d\geq3$, we construct states of maximal Schmidt number satisfying the reduction criterion on both subsystems.

</details>


### [130] [Optimal Quantum Algorithms for Ordered Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34293)
*Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两种新的量子算法解决了有序搜索问题，实现了最优的查询复杂度 1/π·ln n + o(log n)，最终完全确定了量子有序搜索的精确常数因子，解决了这一长期悬而未决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序搜索是在已排序列表中定位目标元素的经典问题。经典二分搜索需要 ⌈log₂ n⌉ 次查询且为最优。虽然已知量子算法可以提供常数因子的加速，但精确的常数因子一直是一个长期悬而未决的公开问题。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白，确定量子有序搜索的精确最优查询复杂度。

Method: 研究提出了两种新的量子算法来实现最优查询复杂度：(1) 第一种算法由 Claude Fable 5 发现，是一种简单的零错误算法，通过问题的连续松弛推导得出；(2) 第二种算法由 GPT-5.6-Sol 发现（受 Claude 零错误算法启发），是基于 Farhi、Goldstone、Gutmann 和 Sipser 的多项式规划解析解的精确算法。

Result: 两种算法均达到了最优的查询复杂度 1/π·ln n + o(log n)，成功实现了量子有序搜索的理论下界。这确立了量子算法相对于经典二分搜索的精确常数因子加速比（约为 log₂ e/π ≈ 0.46）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过提出两种达到理论最优的量子算法，彻底解决了量子有序搜索中精确常数因子的长期开放问题。结果表明最优查询复杂度为 1/π·ln n + o(log n)，为量子搜索算法的理论界限提供了完整的刻画，具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: Ordered search is the problem of locating a target element in a sorted list of size $n$ using comparison queries. Classically, binary search requires $\lceil \log_2 n\rceil$ queries, which is optimal. Quantum algorithms offer a constant-factor speedup, but the precise constant has been a longstanding open question. We close this gap by exhibiting two new quantum algorithms for ordered search, each using the optimal $\frac{1}π\ln n+o(\log n)$ queries. The first, discovered by Claude Fable 5, is a simple zero-error algorithm derived from a continuum relaxation of the problem. The second, discovered by GPT-5.6-Sol (informed by Claude's zero-error algorithm), is an exact algorithm based on an analytic solution of the polynomial program of Farhi, Goldstone, Gutmann, and Sipser.

</details>


### [131] [Observation of a topological edge state among localized bulk states in the anisotropic quantum Rabi model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34332)
*Sungjoo Lim,Chanyang Im,Christopher G. Yale,Brian K. McFarland,Edward C. Tortorici,Daniel S. Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Susan M. Clark,Mahn-Soo Choi,Junki Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在囚禁离子量子模拟器中研究了各向异性量子Rabi模型的拓扑边缘态，发现尽管拓扑边缘态和体态在空间上都表现为局域化分布，但拓扑边缘态具有明确的手征性、近乎完全的自旋-玻色子可分性以及压缩真空态特征（压缩度达6.45 dB），这些内禀拓扑特征可用于区分和表征拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏平移周期性的系统中，体态也会变得局域化，导致无法仅通过空间局域化特性来区分拓扑边缘态与体态。因此需要找到拓扑边缘态的内禀拓扑标识，以便在非周期性系统中准确识别和表征拓扑相。

Method: 利用囚禁离子量子模拟器实验研究各向异性量子Rabi模型（AQRM），该模型在一维合成晶格中具有拓扑相，但其平移对称性被与格点指数成比例的非均匀耦合所破坏。通过测量本征态的手征性、自旋-玻色子可分性，并使用相空间层析技术分析玻色子组分的量子态特性，系统性地表征拓扑边缘态和体态的区别。

Result: 实验发现拓扑边缘态（TES）表现出明确的手征性和近乎完全的自旋-玻色子可分性，这与体态形成鲜明对比。相空间层析进一步揭示拓扑边缘态的玻色子组分是压缩真空态，压缩度高达6.45 dB。这些特征成功地将拓扑边缘态与体态区分开来。

Conclusion: 研究通过内禀拓扑特征（手征性、自旋-玻色子可分性、压缩真空态特性）成功识别了各向异性量子Rabi模型中的拓扑边缘态，证明了本征态层面的表征方法是探测拓扑现象的有效途径，为在非周期性和无平移对称性的系统中研究拓扑相提供了新的实验方案。

Abstract: Topological phases are governed by discrete symmetries that protect boundary modes against local perturbations. When translational periodicity is absent, the bulk states also become localized, so that a topological edge state can no longer be distinguished from them by spatial localization alone. Here, we investigate the topological edge state (TES) and bulk eigenstates of the anisotropic quantum Rabi model (AQRM) in a trapped-ion quantum simulator. The AQRM hosts a topological phase in a one-dimensional synthetic lattice, whose translational symmetry is broken by the non-uniform couplings scaling with the site index. While both the TES and bulk states show localized distributions, we find that the TES exhibits well-defined chirality and near-complete spin--boson separability as signatures of the topological phase, in contrast to the bulk states. Phase-space tomography further reveals that the bosonic component of the TES is a squeezed vacuum state, with squeezing up to 6.45 dB. These results identify the TES through its intrinsic topological signatures and establish eigenstate-level characterization as a route to probing topological phenomena.

</details>


### [132] [Cavity magnonics and bound states in the continuum with Bragg and anti-Bragg mirrors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34365)
*B. -Y. Wu,K. -P. Li,G. Chen,W. -M. Zhou,C. -X. Run,K. -M. Hsieh,A. F. Kockum,Z. -R. Lin,W. Nie,I. -C. Hoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在腔磁振子系统中实验实现了连续谱束缚态(BICs),利用两个周期性铁磁球阵列和探测球构建Bragg和反Bragg腔,通过扫描探测球实现了腔场空间分布的位置依赖性探测,为研究光-物质相互作用的奇异态和混合量子网络提供了灵活的实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续谱束缚态(BICs)是由周期性共振发射体阵列形成的有效腔中的一种特殊量子态,但其空间分布特征的产生和探测一直是实验难题。现有的Bragg镜系统虽然能形成有效腔,但缺乏灵活的探测手段来表征BICs的空间轮廓。为解决这一挑战,需要开发新的实验架构来实现BICs并探测其空间特性。

Method: 采用双开放波导架构,使用两个周期性铁磁球阵列作为镜面,在中间放置探测球,构建腔磁振子系统。实验中实现了两种类型的腔:Bragg腔(半波长间距)和反Bragg腔(不同晶格常数的镜面)。通过移动探测球的位置,进行腔场的空间扫描探测。研究了镜面球形成的超模态(包括亮态和暗态)与探测磁振子的相干耦合,分析了极化激元分裂与探测球尺寸及镜面球数量的标度关系。

Result: 成功在腔磁振子系统中实验观测到连续谱束缚态。在Bragg腔中,发现简并的超模态由镜面球形成,其中单个暗超模态与腔内探测磁振子相干耦合,形成两个极化激元,其分裂强度与探测球尺寸和镜面球数量呈标度关系。反Bragg腔在带隙中存在亮态和暗态超模态,显著改变了磁振子-腔的相互作用方式。通过移动探测球实现了腔场的位置依赖性探测,揭示了BICs在这些腔中的作用。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在腔磁振子系统中实验实现并探测了连续谱束缚态的空间分布,证明了带有扫描探测的灵活实验平台的有效性。该平台不仅可用于探测光-物质相互作用产生的其他奇异态,还可与超导电路接口构建混合量子网络,并为研究Bragg和反Bragg腔中的非厄米物理提供了新途径,为腔量子电动力学和量子信息处理开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: A periodic resonant emitter array coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide can act as a mirror. A typical example is a Bragg mirror, where emitters are spaced by multiples of half wavelengths and collectively enhance light reflection. Two such mirrors form an effective cavity that hosts bound states in the continuum (BICs). However, generating and detecting the spatial profiles of such BICs has proven challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate BICs in cavity magnonics using two periodic ferrimagnetic-sphere arrays and a probe sphere in a dual-open-waveguide architecture. We realize both Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities, whose mirrors have different lattice constants. In the Bragg cavity, there are degenerate supermodes formed by mirror spheres. We show that a single dark supermode coherently couples to the probe magnon in the cavity region, forming two polaritons whose splitting scales with both the probe-sphere size and the number of mirror spheres. By contrast, the anti-Bragg cavity has bright and dark supermodes in a bandgap, substantially changing the magnon-cavity interaction. Moreover, by moving the probe sphere, we perform position-dependent detection of the cavity field, highlighting the role of BICs in these cavities. Our flexible experimental setup with a scanning probe opens possibilities to detect other exotic states created by light-matter interaction, to interface with superconducting circuits in hybrid quantum networks, and to study non-Hermitian physics with Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities.

</details>


### [133] [Experimental Realization and Phase-Space Winding of a Topological Defect State in the Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34389)
*Kyungmin Lee,Jiyong Kang,Sunkyu Yu,Jaehun You,Wonhyeong Choi,Taehyun Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在单个俘获离子系统中实验实现了量子拉比模型的拓扑缺陷态，通过侧带相位调控在Fock态晶格表示下构建可调界面，利用Wigner函数相空间重构观测到缠绕数为1和0的拓扑特征，并通过载波调制动力学探测验证了缺陷态的激发间隙和跃迁通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷态是凝聚态物理中的重要概念，但在量子光学系统中的实验实现和观测仍具挑战性。量子拉比模型作为描述光与物质相互作用的基本模型，其在非均匀合成晶格中的拓扑性质尚未被充分探索。研究者希望利用俘获离子系统的高度可控性，在单离子自旋-玻色子混合系统中实验实现并表征拓扑缺陷态，建立合成晶格拓扑物理与可测量相空间几何之间的联系。

Method: 采用单个俘获的171Yb+离子作为实验平台，利用量子拉比模型中振子固有的数态依赖耦合在Fock态晶格表示下构建可调界面。通过调节侧带相位参数，在不同耦合区域独立制备拓扑缺陷态。使用特征函数层析成像技术重构态的运动相空间分布，获得Wigner函数质心轨迹以提取拓扑缠绕数。通过改变载波振幅对轨迹进行重标度测试，验证拓扑不变性。采用载波振幅调制作为互补的动力学探测手段，测量自旋响应以确定缺陷态的激发能隙和可访问的跃迁通道。

Result: 成功在单离子系统中制备并观测到量子拉比模型的拓扑缺陷态。通过Wigner函数质心轨迹分析，在两个不同耦合区域分别观测到缠绕数为1和0的拓扑相，明确验证了拓扑相变。载波振幅重标度实验显示轨迹可缩放但缠绕数保持不变，证实了拓扑性质的鲁棒性。载波振幅调制动力学探测得到的自旋响应与理论预期的激发能隙和跃迁选择定则一致，进一步验证了缺陷态的本征性质。实验建立了非均匀合成晶格拓扑物理与混合量子系统相空间几何之间的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在单俘获离子平台上实验实现了量子拉比模型的拓扑缺陷态，通过相空间层析成像技术直接观测到不同耦合区域的拓扑缠绕数特征。实验证明了拓扑性质的鲁棒性，并通过动力学探测验证了缺陷态的能谱结构。该工作将合成晶格中的拓扑缺陷态物理与可测量的相空间几何联系起来，为在混合自旋-玻色子系统中探索拓扑量子态提供了新的实验范式，对量子模拟和拓扑量子信息处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We experimentally realize a topological defect state of the quantum Rabi model in a single trapped $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}^{+}$ ion. The state is supported by a tunable interface arising from the oscillator's intrinsic number-dependent coupling in the Fock-state-lattice representation. We independently prepare the state at each sideband-phase setting and reconstruct its motional phase-space distribution using characteristic-function tomography. The resulting Wigner-centroid trajectories exhibit winding numbers of one and zero in the two coupling regimes. Changing the carrier amplitude rescales the trajectories while preserving their windings. Carrier-amplitude modulation provides a complementary dynamical probe, yielding a spin response consistent with the excitation gaps and accessible transition channels from the defect state. The experiment connects topological defect-state physics in a nonuniform synthetic lattice to the measurable phase-space geometry of a hybrid spin-boson system.

</details>


### [134] [Coherence Rather Than Error Rate Governs Privacy in Multi-Tenant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34411)
*Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了商业云量子计算中多租户环境下的信息泄露问题，提出了基于量子态可区分性的信息论度量方法，并在IBM和IQM的商用量子处理器上进行了实验验证，发现传统错误率指标无法反映真实的信息泄露风险，同时提出了基于Pauli随机化的防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业云量子计算采用多租户模式，将量子设备的不同区域租给独立用户，但现有的平均门错误率等标准指标无法准确衡量租户之间的信息泄露程度。由于标准错误度量对相干性错误和随机性错误不敏感，相同基准错误率的边界可能提供显著不同的信息泄露量，这对用户的隐私和安全构成潜在威胁，因此需要建立新的信息泄露评估体系。

Method: 提出了一种基于信息论的信息泄露度量概念,该概念源于量子态可区分性原理。在商用量子设备上进行实验测量:156量子比特的IBM Kingston设备和20量子比特的IQM Garnet设备。采用在受害者整个寄存器上实施的均匀Pauli随机化作为防御机制,理论上该方法不会产生保真度损失。通过对比分析相同基准错误率下不同边界的信息泄露差异,揭示传统错误度量的局限性。

Result: 实验结果表明,具有相同基准错误率的租户边界可以表现出显著不同的信息泄露量,这是因为标准错误度量无法区分相干性错误和随机性错误,而提出的信息泄露概念能够捕捉这一差异。Pauli随机化防御机制理论上可将信息泄露降低到零且不损失保真度,但实验中由于错误累积导致了非平凡的性能退化。在两种商用量子设备上成功测量和量化了跨租户边界的信息泄露。

Conclusion: 研究明确指出,云量子计算提供商应在设备数据表中除了报告标准错误率外,还应报告信息泄露指标,以使用户能够在使用设备前评估隐私和安全风险。传统的平均门错误率等指标不足以保障多租户量子计算环境的安全性。尽管Pauli随机化提供了理论上的完美防御,但实际实现中的错误累积问题仍需解决。该工作为量子云计算的安全标准制定提供了重要参考,呼吁建立更全面的设备性能表征体系。

Abstract: Multi-tenant computing enables providers of commercial cloud quantum processors to rent disjoint sectors of a device to independent users. Average gate error, which cloud quantum computing providers report, does not determine how much one tenant learns about another. We propose an information-theoretic notion of information leakage across co-tenancy boundaries stemming from quantum state distinguishability. We measure this leakage on commercially-available 156-qubit (IBM Kingston) and 20-qubit (IQM Garnet) devices. Boundaries with identical benchmarked error can offer significantly different amount of information leakage because standard reported measures of error are blind to coherent-versus-stochastic nature of the error while the proposed notion of information leakage is not. A uniform Pauli randomisation implemented over the victim's whole register is used as a defence mechanism to reduce the information leakage to zero. The defence theoretically does not incur a fidelity cost, but the experiments show a non-trivial degradation caused by accumulation of errors. We provide a specific call-for-action to the providers of quantum cloud computing to report information leakage in addition to standard error rates in their device datasheet to enable users to compute privacy and security risks prior to engagement with the device.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum Security of XOR of Permutations via Fourier Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34413)
*Wonseok Choi,Minki Hhan,Junyoung Jang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了XOR of Permutations (XoP)构造在量子攻击下具有超越生日界的安全性,证明了r≥2个随机置换的异或在整个查询范围内对q次量子查询算法的不可区分性优势为O(min{q³/2^(rn), q^1.5/2^((r-0.5)n), 1/2^((r-1.5)n)}),远超量子碰撞查找攻击的2^(n/3)界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然XoP构造在经典环境下超越生日界的安全性已被充分建立,但其在量子叠加查询攻击下的安全性一直是一个广泛开放的问题。研究者需要证明基于置换构造的伪随机函数在量子环境下是否仍能保持超越生日界的安全性,这对于后量子密码学具有重要意义。

Method: 本文采用多项式方法的傅里叶分析变体进行证明:1)利用q次量子查询算法的优势可由度数至多2q的傅里叶分量或2q个输入-输出数据控制;2)对大多数分量的范数进行良好界定,证明了2^(-(r-3/2)n)的界;3)将低度分量(如度数2和度数4项)重新解释为其他问题的优势总和,例如带有和不带有planted collisions的随机函数的优势;4)使用Zhandry的小范围分布方法对这些优势进行界定;5)证明了一个新的大范围情况下的小范围不可区分性界,作为独立贡献。

Result: 证明了r≥2个在{0,1}^n上的随机置换的XOR对于任何q次查询的量子算法具有O(min{q³/2^(rn), q^1.5/2^((r-0.5)n), 1/2^((r-1.5)n)})的不可区分性优势界(对所有q≪2^n)。XoP在整个查询范围内保持安全,远超量子碰撞查找攻击导致的2^(n/3)界限。提出了多个启发式攻击,表明在q≤2^(n/2)和q≈2^n范围内界限的紧致性。这是首个从置换构造中实现量子版本超越生日界安全性的构造。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明了XoP构造在量子攻击环境下达到了超越生日界的安全性,填补了该领域的重要空白。通过傅里叶分析的多项式方法和对低度分量的巧妙重新解释,成功建立了严格的安全性界限。这一结果表明基于置换的伪随机函数构造在后量子时代仍然可行,为设计抗量子攻击的密码原语提供了理论基础。同时,文中证明的大范围小范围不可区分性新界限具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: The XOR of two or more independent random permutations (XoP) is the prototypical pseudorandom function built from permutations achieving security beyond the birthday bound. The classical security of the XoP construction is well established, but its security against quantum attacks that query XoP in superposition has remained widely open.
  We prove that the XOR of $r\ge 2$ random permutations over $\{0,1\}^n$ is indistinguishable from a random function by any $q$-query quantum algorithm with advantage \[O\left(\min\left\{\frac{q^3}{2^{rn}},\frac{q^{1.5}}{2^{(r-0.5)n}},\frac{1}{2^{(r-1.5)n}}\right\}\right)\] for all $q\ll 2^n$. In particular, XoP remains secure throughout the entire query range, far beyond the $2^{n/3}$ bound due to quantum collision finding attacks. This is the first construction from permutations that achieves the quantum version of the beyond birthday bound security. We also present several heuristic attacks suggesting the tightness of our bounds in ranges $q\le 2^{n/2}$ and $\approx 2^n$.
  We use a Fourier-analytic variant of the polynomial method: the advantage of any $q$-query quantum algorithm is controlled by the Fourier components of degree at most $2q$, or by $2q$ input-output data of the construction. The norms of most components are bounded well, proving the bound $2^{-(r-3/2)n}$.
  The norm of low-degree components turn out to be too large for the bounds $q^3/2^{rn}$ and $q^{1.5}/2^{(r-0.5)n}$. We reinterpret these low-degree components as (sums of) advantages of the other problems. For example, the degree-2 and degree-4 terms are interpreted as the advantages against random functions with and without \emph{planted collisions}, which in turn are bounded using Zhandry's small-range distributions. Along the way, we prove a new bound for the small-range indistinguishability for (ironically) large ranges, which is of independent interest.

</details>


### [136] [Excitation Gap of a Confined Chiral Mode Measures Critical Zero-Point Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34416)
*Shu He,Liwei Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子Rabi环中均匀与手性超辐射边界交汇处的能隙标度行为，发现在四阶耦合作用下能隙遵循η^(-2/3)标度律，并通过四阶薛定谔方程和自旋-光子对角化验证了该理论，同时揭示了具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换作用的三角自旋系统满足相同关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子Rabi环系统中，当均匀超辐射态与手性超辐射态在边界处相遇时，二阶理论会留下具有有限径向宽度的简并手性阶梯态。然而，二阶理论无法完整描述该边界区域的物理行为，特别是能隙的标度规律。因此需要引入更高阶（四阶）的耦合效应来准确描述这一临界区域的量子态结构和能隙闭合行为，并揭示其与原子-腔频率比η之间的依赖关系。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：(1) 在二阶理论基础上引入手性量子态，分析其对径向波函数展宽的影响；(2) 考虑四阶耦合项对均匀二阶系数的提升作用；(3) 推导四阶薛定谔方程来确定能隙、零点方差等可观测量及其比值的首阶修正；(4) 通过完整的自旋-光子哈密顿量对角化进行数值验证；(5) 将该理论框架推广至具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换作用的三角集体自旋系统；(6) 分析三坐标谱结构，建立与相邻标度律（η^(-1)和η^(-1/3)）以及光子数饱和现象的联系。

Result: 研究获得以下主要结果：(1) 能隙正比于均匀零点方差除以原子-腔频率比η，具体遵循η^(-2/3)的标度律闭合；(2) 零点方差随η^(1/3)增长；(3) 四阶薛定谔方程成功预测了能隙、方差及其比值的首阶修正项；(4) 完整自旋-光子对角化数值计算证实了理论预测；(5) 具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换的三角自旋系统满足相同的标度关系，但系数不同且修正项符号相反；(6) 量子Rabi环的三坐标能谱结构将该η^(-2/3)能隙与邻近的η^(-1)和η^(-1/3)标度律以及光子数饱和现象有机联系起来。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了量子Rabi环中均匀与手性超辐射边界交汇处的精细量子结构，证明了四阶耦合效应导致能隙遵循η^(-2/3)标度律，而零点方差按η^(1/3)增长。四阶薛定谔方程为该临界区域提供了准确的理论描述，并通过数值对角化得到验证。该理论框架可推广至其他具有类似拓扑结构的量子多体系统，如Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya三角自旋系统。研究建立了不同标度区域之间的联系，完善了对量子坂环超辐射相变临界行为的理解，为超强耦合光-物质相互作用系统的量子相变研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Where uniform and chiral superradiant boundaries meet in a quantum Rabi ring, the quadratic theory leaves a degenerate chiral ladder with finite radial width. We show that adding one chiral quantum widens the ladder's radial wave function; the quartic coupling then raises the uniform quadratic coefficient. The resulting gap is proportional to the uniform zero-point variance divided by the atomic-to-cavity frequency ratio $η$: it closes as $η^{-2/3}$ while the variance grows as $η^{1/3}$. A quartic Schrödinger equation fixes both observables and the first correction to their ratio, as confirmed by full spin-photon diagonalization. A triangle of collective spins with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya exchange obeys the same relation, with a different coefficient and a correction of opposite sign. The ring's three-coordinate spectrum connects this gap to the neighboring $η^{-1}$ and $η^{-1/3}$ laws and to photon-number saturation.

</details>


### [137] [Near-Optimal Bounds on the Density of Low-Energy States of $k$-Local Hamiltonians and Faster Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34532)
*Sevag Gharibian,François Le Gall,Ranitha Mataraarachchi,Suguru Tamaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了更快的指数量子算法用于一般k-局部哈密顿量的低能估计和态制备问题,通过二元熵函数控制运行时间指数,在特定参数范围内相比已有工作将指数改进了log(k/ε)倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能估计和态制备是量子复杂性理论中的基本挑战。虽然最近的工作突破了自然的Grover界O*(2^(n/2)),但其改进依赖于相对精度ε和局部性k。本文旨在进一步加速这些问题的量子算法,特别是在ε/k较小时获得更优的指数级改进。

Method: 核心技术是通过对哈密顿量基态进行去极化(depolarizing)操作,建立由熵控制的哈密顿量低能子空间维度的下界。该方法利用二元熵函数来控制算法的运行时间指数。对于固定的局部性k,该下界在指数上达到了常数因子范围内的最优性。同一框架还可应用于任意交互图上的Heisenberg模型、XY模型和Ising模型。

Result: 对于足够小的ε/k值,新算法相比BGLGST(PRL 2025)的工作将运行时间指数改进了log(k/ε)倍。建立的低能子空间维度下界对于固定k在指数上是最优的(相差常数因子)。该方法成功推广到多种特定模型,为Heisenberg、XY和Ising模型提供了更紧的界。

Conclusion: 本文通过熵控制的方法显著提升了k-局部哈密顿量低能估计和态制备问题的量子算法效率,在特定参数区域实现了指数级的改进。所提出的基于去极化技术的维度下界框架具有理论最优性,并可推广应用于多种物理模型,为量子复杂性理论提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: Low-energy estimation and state preparation for general $k$-local Hamiltonians are fundamental challenges in quantum complexity theory. Buhrman et al.~ [BGLGST, PRL 2025] recently broke the natural Grover bound $O^\ast(2^{n/2})$ for both problems, with the improvement depending on the relative accuracy $\varepsilon$ and the locality $k$. In this work, we present faster exponential quantum algorithms for these problems, where the binary entropy function governs the runtime exponent. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon/k$, our algorithms improve the exponent by a factor of $\log(k/\varepsilon)$ over [BGLGST, PRL 2025]. Our main technical result is an entropy-governed lower bound on the dimension of the Hamiltonian's low-energy subspace, obtained by depolarizing its ground state. For fixed $k$, this bound is optimal up to constant factors in the exponent. The same framework yields tighter bounds for Heisenberg, $XY$, and Ising models on arbitrary interaction graphs.

</details>
